We study the construction \emph{rather than} in ACL papers from 2019, ACL-style arXiv papers from 2026, and papers written by GPT models from the same titles and abstracts. Its rate in 2026 is seven times the 2019 rate, and higher still in the GPT papers. Two annotators, blind to the source, find almost no 2019 use \emph{annoying} and about one in ten 2026 uses; they seldom agree on which, yet about half of 2026 papers contain a use that annoys each of them. \emph{Annoying} uses present the rejected alternative less favorably than legitimate uses. Raters of preference data and open reward models favor the construction, and an instruction to be honest promotes it. We conjecture that it is a side effect of post-training on pairwise preferences, which credit a disavowal in a single response and cannot register its cost across a text.

</details>


### [208] [Mechanics of Long-Context Hybrid Models Part 1.1: From Hybrid Attention to Hybrid Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10114)
*Xiaoran Liu,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"长上下文混合模型力学"框架，作为系列第一部分，研究全注意力与滑动窗口注意力(SWA)或门控线性注意力(LA，如GLA、GDN)的混合模型。作者发现"跷跷板效应"：LA混合模型更受益于长上下文持续预训练，SWA混合模型在长度外推上表现更好；并提出SWA的"短上下文学习陷阱"等问题及LA的"位置外推马太效应"，进而提出滑动窗口线性注意力，实现16倍免训练长度外推并在64k NIAH-SK1上保持100%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM架构正从纯全注意力转向混合模型以提升长上下文效率、长度外推与上下文扩展性能，但缺乏对混合模型为何有效及如何更好设计的机理性解释。本文旨在揭示不同注意力模块混合后的内在机制。

Method: 以全注意力与SWA或门控线性注意力(GLA、GDN)的混合模型为对象，分析其在长度外推与长上下文持续预训练中的表现差异。归因于不同注意力机制诱导的位置归纳偏置差异；刻画SWA混合模型的"短上下文学习陷阱""短窗口疲劳""长窗口惰性"，以及LA混合模型的"位置外推马太效应"，并提出滑动窗口线性注意力。

Result: 观察到"上下文扩展跷跷板效应"：LA混合模型更受益于长上下文持续预训练，SWA混合模型在长度外推下表现更好。SWA混合模型需扩展窗口以提升持续预训练性能。提出的滑动窗口线性注意力实现16倍免训练长度外推，并在64k上下文长度的NIAH-SK1上保持100%准确率。

Conclusion: 混合模型的行为差异源于注意力机制诱导的位置归纳偏置，理解这些机制有助于更优设计。滑动窗口线性注意力可同时兼顾长度外推与长上下文性能，为混合模型设计提供新方向。

Abstract: The architectural design of Large Language Models (LLMs) is shifting from traditional full-attention-only models to hybrid models, which combine different attention modules to improve long-context efficiency and performance in length extrapolation and context extension. To explain why hybrid models work and how to design them better, we propose Mechanics of Long-Context Hybrid Models. As Part 1.1 of this series, we begin with hybrids of full attention and either sliding-window attention (SWA) or gated variants of linear attention (LA), represented by GLA and GDN. We first observe a Seesaw Effect in Context Extension: LA hybrids benefit more from long-context continual pretraining, whereas SWA hybrids perform better under length extrapolation. We attribute this behavior to differences in the positional inductive biases induced by these attention mechanisms. We find that SWA hybrids suffer from a Short-Context Learning Trap, Short-Window Weariness, and Long-Window Laziness, and require extended windows to enhance performance in continual long-context pretraining. For LA hybrids, we summarize the Matthew Effect of Hybrid Position Extrapolation and propose Sliding-Window Linear Attention, achieving 16$\times$ training-free length extrapolation while maintaining 100\% accuracy on NIAH-SK1 in 64k context length.

</details>


### [209] [LLM4Impact: Integrating Heterogeneous Information for Scientific Impact Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10138)
*Yong Cao,Markus Flicke,Haoyu He,Katrin Renz,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM4Impact：面向科学影响预测的证据感知方法，融合语义、图、LLM 与时间表征，通过上下文门控与校准模块自适应整合异构信息，在200万篇论文基准上一致优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测新发表论文的未来影响具有挑战性，因其只能依据发表时刻可得的异构信息进行推断。现有方法要么依赖单一信息源，要么简单组合多源信息，却未考虑不同信息源在预测中的差异化作用。

Method: 提出 LLM4Impact，一种证据感知的科学影响预测方法。它联合语义、图、LLM 和时间四类表征，通过连续前缀 token 将图信息注入冻结的 LLM；采用上下文感知门控机制自适应地为不同证据加权；并使用独立校准模块处理引用尺度上的领域与时间差异。同时构建大规模基准数据集：200万篇论文、防泄漏的时点异构 ego 图、时间划分，以及年级与月级引用目标。

Result: LLM4Impact 持续优于语义、图与 LLM 类强基线：同分布测试集年份 RMSE 降低 10.13%，域外分布下降低 6.87%。结果表明证据价值具有上下文依赖性——不同论文受益于不同来源，领域与发表时间也影响证据向引用的转化。

Conclusion: 该发现支持自适应证据选择与上下文条件化校准，而非单纯追求更丰富的表征。作者将在发表后公开代码、基准数据集及交互式网页演示。

Abstract: Predicting the future impact of a newly published paper is challenging because it must be inferred from heterogeneous evidence available at publication time. Existing approaches often rely on a single source of information or combine multiple sources without accounting for their different predictive roles. In this paper, we present LLM4Impact, an evidence-aware method for scientific impact prediction that learns to represent, integrate, and calibrate heterogeneous information. LLM4Impact combines semantic, graph, LLM, and temporal representations, and injects graph information into a frozen LLM through continuous prefix tokens. A context aware gating mechanism adaptively weights different evidence, while a separate calibration module accounts for domain and temporal variation in citation scales. We further construct a large-scale benchmark dataset with 2 million papers, leakage-safe point-in-time heterogeneous ego graphs, temporal splits, and both year-level and month-level citation targets. Experiments show that LLM4Impact consistently outperforms strong semantic, graph, and LLM based baselines, with a 10.13% reduction in year RMSE on the in distribution test set and a 6.87% reduction under out-of-domain distribution. Our results reveal that the value of such evidence is context dependent: different papers benefit from different sources, while domain and publication time affect how evidence translates into citations. This finding motivates adaptive evidence selection and context-conditioned calibration rather than simply richer representations. We will release our code, benchmark, and an interactive web demonstration upon publication.

</details>


### [210] [InterView-C: A Synchronized Multimodal Corpus of VR Avatar-Mediated Survey Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10145)
*Patrick Schrottenbacher,Leon Hammerla,Lydia Kleine,Doris Stingl,Alexander Mehler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了InterView-C：一个完全在虚拟现实中进行的德语多模态访谈语料库（27场访谈、54段录音），将语音与凝视、头部/身体动作、面部行为、手势等同步，并提供词级对齐的人工校订转写、题项时间、问卷应答及否定线索/范围标注，实证展示了ASR转写偏差与否定模型迁移困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以文本为主的NLP方法与多模态口语互动之间存在接口缺口：自动语音转写可能扭曲语言相关特征，基于既有资源训练的模型在转写口语数据上还面临迁移挑战，因此需要既保留多模态行为又提供可靠语言标注的资源。

Method: 构建InterView-C：27场完全在VR中进行的德语调查访谈，访谈双方均以虚拟形象呈现；采集并同步语音互动与行为数据（凝视、头部与身体运动、面部行为、手部与手指追踪）；提供参考转写与语言学标注，包括全部54段录音的词级带时、人工后编辑逐字转写，题项时间、问卷应答，以及1,422句的否定线索与范围标注（其中1,398句双人标注）。

Result: 标注一致性：线索α=0.87，范围α=0.81。实证显示两类挑战：9个开放权重ASR系统对简短封闭式回答和数字词存在不成比例的误识；在既有语料上训练的否定模型在转写访谈上表现更低且波动大，而基于InterView-C标注训练的模型表现更好。

Conclusion: InterView-C支持对口语互动进行语言学研究，同时保持其与丰富多模态行为的对齐，为文本NLP与多模态口语互动之间提供可靠接口。

Abstract: We present InterView-C, a German multimodal corpus of 27 survey interviews conducted entirely in virtual reality, with both interlocutors represented by avatars. The corpus aligns spoken interaction with synchronized behavioral data, including gaze, head and body movement, facial behavior, hand and finger tracking. Its reference transcripts and linguistic annotations provide a reliable interface between this multimodal spoken interaction and predominantly text-based NLP methods. This interface is important because automatically transcribing speech can distort linguistically relevant information, while downstream models trained on existing resources may additionally face transfer challenges when applied to transcribed spoken data. InterView-C therefore provides word-timed and manually post-edited verbatim transcripts for all 54 recordings, interview-item timings, questionnaire responses and negation cue and scope annotations for 1,422 sentences, 1,398 of them doubly annotated (α=0.87 for cues; α=0.81 for scopes). We demonstrate both challenges empirically: nine open-weight ASR systems disproportionately misrecognize short closed answers and number words, while negation models trained on existing corpora show lower and highly variable performance on our transcribed interviews than a model trained on the InterView-C annotations. InterView-C thus enables linguistic analyses of spoken interaction while retaining their alignment with rich multimodal behavior.

</details>


### [211] [HySPE: Positional Encoding via Symplectic Dual Shears](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10154)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双曲辛位置编码HySPE，利用Sp(2,R)的双曲分支做位置注意力，实现长度无关的数值界与强零样本外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE仅参数化Sp(2,R)的紧致椭圆分支（旋转），在超长序列零样本外推时数值漂移、困惑度急剧退化；需要探索非紧致辛变换分支以获得更稳健的位置编码。

Method: HySPE通过双重剪切（dual shears）的阻尼对称组合实现Sp(2,R)双曲分支，得到每通道对具两个谱衰减率的共形辛收缩；为消除朴素绝对分解的指数表示漂移，在不变特征基中对算子对角化，并引入分块坐标重基（blockwise coordinate rebasing）与自适应中心化执行。

Result: 数值界与序列长度无关，前向延迟与缓存RoPE相当（RTX 4090上7.21 ms）。TinyShakespeare上HySPE-UltraLong在16倍零样本外推（L=4096）保持困惑度4.810，RoPE退化至131.198；WikiText-103上51M参数子词Transformer（训练长度512）域内与RoPE相当，外推至8192时尾部困惑度较RoPE降低83.9%。

Conclusion: 受控实验表明HySPE具备外推鲁棒性与数值稳定性；在大规模基础模型上的扩展行为评估是未来重要方向。

Abstract: We introduce Hyperbolic Symplectic Positional Encoding (HySPE), grounding positional attention in non-compact symplectic transformations. While canonical Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) parameterizes the compact, elliptic branch of $\Sp(2,\R)$ via rotations, HySPE operationalizes its hyperbolic branch via a damped symmetric composition of dual shears, yielding a conformally symplectic contraction with two spectral decay rates per channel pair. To eliminate the exponential representation drift inherent to naive absolute factorizations, we diagonalize the operator in its invariant eigenbasis and introduce blockwise coordinate rebasing with adaptive centered execution. This guarantees length-independent numerical bounds while matching cached RoPE forward latency (7.21\,ms on an RTX 4090). On TinyShakespeare, HySPE-UltraLong maintains an invariant perplexity of 4.810 up to $16\times$ zero-shot extrapolation ($L=4096$), whereas RoPE degrades to 131.198. Scaled to a 51M-parameter subword Transformer on WikiText-103 ($L_{\text{train}}=512$), HySPE closely matches RoPE in-domain while robustly extrapolating to length 8192, reducing tail perplexity by 83.9\% over RoPE. While these controlled experiments establish HySPE's extrapolation robustness and numerical stability, evaluating its scaling behavior on large-scale foundation models remains an important direction for future investigation.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Constructing and Assigning Retrieval Credit for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10179)
*Wenyu Huang,Xinyu Hou,Pavlos Vougiouklis,Ruofei Lai,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究中间监督如何改善搜索智能体的强化学习，提出结合中间信号与最终结果奖励的训练框架，实验表明能提升搜索智能体整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）用于训练搜索智能体时，依赖稀疏的结果监督，导致信用分配困难、学习效率受限。作者希望探究中间监督能否缓解这一问题。

Method: 系统研究多种奖励塑形与信用分配策略，从中间检索步骤提供学习信号；在此基础上构建训练框架，将中间信号与最终结果奖励相结合，以改进多步搜索轨迹的学习。

Result: 在匹配训练条件下，于多个基准上实验，搜索智能体的整体性能获得提升；结果表明中间信号的选择及其信用分配位置都会影响训练行为。

Conclusion: 奖励设计与信用分配是训练有效搜索智能体的重要设计维度。

Abstract: Search agents enable Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve and use information for complex multi-hop questions. Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising approach for post-training such agents, but its reliance on sparse, outcome-based supervision can make credit assignment difficult and limit learning efficiency. In this paper, we systematically investigate how intermediate supervision can improve reinforcement learning for search agents. We study a range of reward-shaping and credit-assignment strategies that provide learning signals from intermediate retrieval steps. Building on these insights, we develop a training framework that combines intermediate signals with final outcome rewards to improve learning from multi-step search trajectories. Experiments across multiple benchmarks under matched training conditions demonstrate improvements in aggregate search-agent performance and show that both the choice of intermediate signal and where its credit is assigned affect training behaviour. These findings show that reward design and credit assignment are important design dimensions for training effective search agents.

</details>


### [213] [LLM Persuasion Is in the Eye of the Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10232)
*Kamile Dementaviciute,Julija Vaitonyte,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将9种自动化说服力评估方法统一到同一实验设置下，对15个LLM进行评测，发现方法间排名一致性很弱（平均Spearman ρ=0.25），主因是模型的"选择性拒答"与"通用能力"差异，说明单一说服力分数只在其自身任务设定下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的说服能力已可比肩或超越人类专家，既能用于教育、健康传播等有益场景，也可能被用于操纵和误导，因此评测其说服能力对开发者和监管者日益重要。但现有评测高度碎片化：各研究对"说服"的定义不同，宽泛结论常建立在狭窄、情境化的评估之上。自动化方法因可规模化运行于同一批模型、并涵盖难以或不适合在人类身上测试的高风险说服形式，成为直接比较这些评测的可行途径。

Method: 作者选取9种已发表的自动化说服评测方法，将其适配到统一的实验设置中，在同一批15个LLM上运行，并检验这些方法给出的模型排名是否一致及其原因。分析中重点考察两类因素：一是模型的选择性拒答（对部分任务拒答而对另一些不拒答）对一致性的影响；二是模型通用能力与不同类型说服方法（理性/非操纵性 vs. 操纵性）之间的关系。可分析的完整方法为8种。

Result: 各方法间一致性很弱，平均Spearman ρ=0.25。模型的"有选择性拒答"（直接或间接）使一致性下降约四分之一，且拒答主要集中在操纵类任务上。通用能力也起作用：多数理性说服（非操纵性）方法能追踪通用能力，而多数操纵类方法则不能。整体上，一致性更多取决于方法所设定的任务，而非其打分方式；但受限于仅8种可分析方法，该结论仅为提示性。更一般地，说服力分数混合了模型的"说服能力"与"说服意愿"。

Conclusion: 单一说服力分数只对其自身任务设定有信息量，难以代表模型跨任务的整体说服力。评估应明确区分模型的"能说服"（ability）与"愿说服"（willingness），并对操纵类与理性类任务分别报告；同时需要更多可统一比较的方法来验证上述模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have already been shown to match or exceed human experts in persuasion. While their persuasive capabilities hold promise for beneficial uses such as education and health communication, they can also be used to manipulate and misinform, making their evaluation a growing priority for developers and regulators. That evaluation, however, remains fragmented: studies differ in what they treat as persuasion, and broad claims often rest on narrow, situation-specific assessments. Automated methods, often modelled on human studies, offer a way to compare such assessments directly, as they can be run on the same models at scale and can include high-risk forms of persuasion that would be difficult or unethical to test on people. In this study, we adapt nine published automated methods to a shared setup, run them on the same fifteen LLMs, and ask whether their rankings agree and why. We find that the methods agree only weakly (mean Spearman $ρ= 0.25$). Our analyses point to two contributing factors. Models that refuse some tasks but not others, directly or indirectly, lower agreement by about a quarter, and these refusals fall mostly on manipulation tasks. General capability also plays a part: most rational persuasion (non-manipulative) methods track it, whereas most manipulation methods do not. Together, these findings suggest that agreement depends more on the task a method sets than on how it scores persuasion, although this pattern is only indicative given the eight methods available for analysis. More broadly, our results suggest that persuasion scores combine a model's ability to persuade with its willingness to do so. A single score is therefore informative about its own setting, but says little about a model's persuasiveness across tasks.

</details>


### [214] [Nobody Truly Agrees on Sentiment: Humans, Bespoke Tools, and LLMs Struggle with Social Media Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10318)
*Himarsha R. Jayanetti,Sivakanesan Dhanushkanda,Shuai Hao,Michael L. Nelson,Michele C. Weigle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对3种情感分析工具（TextBlob、VADER、Twitter-roBERTa-base）与3种大语言模型在100条推文上与6名人工标注者进行标注者间信度评估，发现即便人类标注者之间也仅呈中等一致，Twitter-roBERTa-base与人类对齐最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体情感分析工具被广泛使用，但其局限性常被忽视。作者希望系统评估不同工具（含新兴LLM）在社交媒体文本上的标注者间信度，明确其在可靠性上的不足及领域微调的必要性。

Method: 以100条推文为样本，比较3种专用情感分析工具（TextBlob、VADER、Twitter-roBERTa-base）和3种大语言模型（Qwen3-32B、GPT-OSS-120B、Llama-4-Maverick-17B）与6名人类标注者的一致性。采用Cohen's kappa进行两两比较、Fleiss' kappa进行多标注者比较，并对比二分类（负向vs非负向、正向vs非正向）与三分类下的表现。

Result: 人类标注者之间仅呈fair一致，体现情感分析的主观性；二分类一致性普遍高于三分类。Twitter-roBERTa-base与人类评分对齐最强，优于专用工具和LLM，尤其在区分负向与非负向方面。LLM彼此间一致性较高，与人类呈中等至较高一致，在正向vs非正向分类上表现更好。

Conclusion: 领域特定微调对可靠的社交媒体情感分析仍至关重要，且以人为本的评估对于建立金标准标签不可或缺。

Abstract: Social media is a rich source of real-time public sentiment, but widely used sentiment analysis tools are often applied without understanding their limitations. In this study, we evaluate the inter-rater reliability of three bespoke sentiment analysis tools (TextBlob, VADER, and Twitter-roBERTa-base) and three large language models (LLMs: Qwen3-32B, GPT-OSS-120B, Llama-4-Maverick-17B) against six human raters across 100 tweets. We measured agreement using two statistical measures: Cohen's kappa for pairwise comparisons and Fleiss' kappa for multiple raters. Even among the human raters, our results showed only fair agreement, highlighting the subjectivity of sentiment analysis. Higher agreement was observed under the binary sentiment classification (negative vs. non-negative and positive vs. non-positive) than under the three-class classification across both humans and automated tools. The Twitter-roBERTa-base model showed the strongest alignment with human ratings, outperforming both bespoke sentiment tools and LLMs, particularly in distinguishing negative versus non-negative sentiment. LLMs showed substantial agreement among themselves and moderate to substantial alignment with humans, performing better in positive vs. non-positive classifications. Our findings underscore that domain-specific fine-tuning remains crucial for reliable social media sentiment analysis, and human-centered evaluation remains essential for establishing gold-standard labels.

</details>


### [215] [Learning to Act with Task Progress: Distilling Small Agents from Compact Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10332)
*Wenxi Gan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出任务进度蒸馏（TPD）离线方法：用简短阶段标签替代冗长推理，训练小模型智能体，在ALFWorld上以少量演示达到甚至超过推理监督，显式阶段标签在中等演示预算下带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用大模型演示训练小智能体可避免每步调用大模型，但关键在于从含推理、动作和任务进展信息的教师轨迹中保留什么。作者希望用更紧凑的监督信号替代冗长推理，提升小模型的学习效率。

Method: TPD将每条演示动作与一个描述当前任务阶段的简短标签配对；学生模型学习这些紧凑目标，并对可接受的"阶段—动作"对联合打分来选动作，再由确定性执行器在环境中执行。与"仅动作监督"和"推理训练+受限动作选择"两种基线对比。

Result: ALFWorld上，1.7B学生用404条演示，TPD与仅动作监督均达72.4%未见任务平均成功率，推理训练基线仅48.3%；200条演示时，显式阶段标签把成功率从48.0%提升到67.7%；演示增至808条时，仅动作学生追平，两者均达76.9%。共享历史分析显示TPD优势部分来自子目标切换（尤其从物体获取到处理）时决策更好。

Conclusion: 紧凑监督足以训练有效的小型任务智能体；显式任务进度标签在中等演示预算下提供额外指导，但随演示增多其优势消失。

Abstract: Learning from large-model demonstrations offers a way to train small agents that can complete recurring tasks without calling a large model at every step. A central design choice is what to retain from teacher trajectories that contain reasoning, actions, and information about task progress. We introduce Task-Progress Distillation (TPD), an offline approach that pairs each demonstrated action with a short label describing the current task stage. The student learns these compact targets and selects actions by jointly scoring admissible stage--action pairs, which a deterministic harness executes in the environment. On ALFWorld, a 1.7B student trained with 404 demonstrations achieves 72.4\% mean unseen task success with either TPD or action-only supervision, compared with 48.3\% for a reasoning-trained student using constrained action selection. Explicit stages provide an additional benefit at 200 demonstrations, improving success from 48.0\% to 67.7\% over action-only supervision. With more demonstrations, the action-only student closes the gap, and both approaches reach 76.9\% at 808 demonstrations. Shared-history analyses link part of TPD's local advantage to better decisions when moving between subgoals, particularly from object acquisition to processing. These results show that compact supervision can train effective small task agents, while explicit task progress provides additional guidance at an intermediate demonstration budget.

</details>


### [216] [Document-Level Text Simplification in Estonian Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10378)
*Meeri-Ly Muru,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估五种多语种大语言模型在爱沙尼亚语篇章级简化任务上的表现，比较三种提示策略，并提出新的篇章连贯性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 篇章级文本简化需处理语篇连贯、指代消解与跨段落一致性，超越句内编辑。高资源语言的句级简化已有进展，但形态丰富、低资源语言（如爱沙尼亚语）的篇章级简化几乎未被探索，存在明显研究空白。

Method: 对五种先进多语种大语言模型在爱沙尼亚语篇章级简化任务上进行综合评估；考察三种提示策略：单次生成、基于流水线的模块化智能体、以及指南增强的流水线。评估框架结合自动指标（可读性、语义保持、语篇连贯）与结构化人工标注协议。

Result: Gemini-2.0 与 LLaMA-3.3 的输出接近母语流畅度且语义保持强；其他模型存在明显的语法与语义局限。

Conclusion: 本工作贡献了新的篇章级连贯性指标、基于证据的提示策略，以及可公开获取的可复现资源。

Abstract: Document-level text simplification involves transformations that go beyond sentence-internal edits, addressing discourse coherence, anaphora resolution, and cross-paragraph consistency. Despite advances in sentence-level simplification for high-resource languages, document-level simplification in morphologically rich, low-resource languages such as Estonian remains largely unexplored. This study presents a comprehensive evaluation of five state-of-the-art multilingual large language models (LLMs) for document-level simplification in Estonian. Three prompting strategies are examined: single-pass generation, pipeline-based modular agents, and guideline-augmented pipelines. The evaluation framework integrates automatic metrics assessing readability, semantic preservation, and discourse coherence, alongside a structured manual annotation protocol. The findings indicate that Gemini-2.0 and LLaMA-3.3 produce outputs with near-native fluency and strong meaning preservation, whereas other models display notable grammatical and semantic limitations. This work contributes novel document-level coherence metrics, evidence-based prompting strategies, and publicly available resources for reproducibility.

</details>


### [217] [CoTrace: Data Recipes for Training Terminal Agents with Harness-Model Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10426)
*Jixuan Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Qinyuan Ye,Yada Pruksachatkun,Haoxiang Zhang,Jingming Zhuo,Yifan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Juntao Tan,Xiangyu Peng,Silvio Savarese,Zeyuan Chen,Lianhui Qin,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出交替式协同演化框架与CoTrace数据配方，按harness来源对轨迹进行路由与匹配，使性能提升至88-90个任务，并证明跨环境迁移取决于运行时的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体能力同时取决于模型权重与运行时harness（负责提示格式化、工具绑定与错误恢复）。现有harness-模型协同演化方法虽同时改进二者，却把harness搜索中产生的轨迹当作无差别的回放缓冲区，忽视了轨迹的训练价值依赖其生成时所处的harness这一事实。

Method: 建立交替式协同演化框架，通过组件级晋升决策解耦harness搜索与策略训练；提出harness感知的数据配方CoTrace，显式管理轨迹路由、来源匹配与课程刷新：由重复出现的执行失败引导harness合成，策略训练严格基于经校验的rollout——与所选运行时匹配的轨迹用于SFT，新在线交互用于RL。

Result: 在Tmax晋升划分上，CoTrace使Qwen3.5-9B在SFT下从78提升到88个已解决任务，在线RL变体达到90；紧凑的harness匹配语料以远低于跨同类harness汇集的更大语料的计算量带来稳定增益。在Terminal-Bench 2.1与SWE-bench Lite上，分布外迁移根本上取决于harness兼容性，训练与评估运行时保持一致可避免在异构脚手架下出现的流程执行崩溃。

Conclusion: 轨迹的训练价值与生成它的harness强耦合，按harness来源进行数据路由与运行时匹配，能以更低计算代价获得更稳定的性能提升，且harness兼容性是分布外迁移的关键前提。

Abstract: Terminal-agent capability depends jointly on model weights and the runtime harness that formats prompts, binds tools, and handles error recovery. Existing harness-model co-evolution approaches improve both components, yet often treat trajectories produced during harness search as an undifferentiated replay buffer. This practice overlooks that a trajectory's value for model training depends on the harness under which it was generated. To systematically analyze this interface, we establish an alternating co-evolution framework that decouples harness search and policy training through component-wise promotion decisions. Within this framework, we introduce CoTrace, a harness-aware data recipe that explicitly governs trajectory routing, provenance matching, and curriculum refresh. Under CoTrace, recurring execution failures guide harness synthesis, while policy training is strictly conditioned on verified rollouts matched to the adopted runtime for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or fresh online interactions for reinforcement learning (RL). On the Tmax promotion split, CoTrace advances Qwen3.5-9B from 78 to 88 solved tasks under supervised fine-tuning while an online reinforcement variant reaches 90. Specifically, a compact harness-matched corpus produces steady model gains at substantially lower compute than much larger corpora pooled across sibling harnesses. Furthermore, evaluations on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-bench Lite show that out-of-distribution transfer depends fundamentally on harness compatibility, where maintaining consistency between training and evaluation runtimes prevents procedural execution breakdowns observed under foreign scaffolds.

</details>


### [218] [PHRBench: A Behavioral Evaluation of Post-Hallucination Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455)
*Linghao Meng,Feng He,Xuan Yang,Junyuan Mao,Pinze Ren,Deqing Mu,Hesen Yang,Qiankun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PHRBench，一个覆盖 4 个领域、18 个模型的受控基准，从行为层面刻画大模型在幻觉前提下的后续推理，将每条推理轨迹独立于最终答案正确性进行结构化分类，并发现成功纠错较罕见、与推理过程中的信念更新频率相关，且幻觉提示的属性对成功恢复具有预测力（AUROC 0.847）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉信息会在多阶段 LLM 系统中传播，成为后续推理的上下文。现有关于"幻觉后推理"（Post-Hallucination Reasoning, PHR）的研究主要刻画最终结果变化与聚合推理动态，对模型在响应层面如何消解幻觉前提的理解不足。

Method: 提出 PHRBench 受控基准，覆盖 4 个领域、18 个 LLM，共 4820 个受控实例。对每条推理轨迹独立于最终答案正确性进行行为结构化刻画，定义三类行为：幻觉顺从（Hallucination Compliance）、幻觉规避（Hallucination Avoidance）、启发式纠正（Heuristic Correction）；并定义"有洞察力的轨迹"为最终成功纠正并得到正确答案的情形。同时构建轻量预测器，利用幻觉提示属性预测成功恢复。

Result: 在 4820 个受控实例上，成功恢复相对罕见；成功恢复与推理轨迹中更频繁的信念更新（belief updates）相关。幻觉提示的属性对成功恢复具有显著预测信号，轻量预测器达到 AUROC 0.847。

Conclusion: 研究提供了幻觉后推理的行为视角，刻画了 LLM 如何消解错误上下文以及何时可能发生成功恢复。

Abstract: Hallucinated information can propagate through multi-stage LLM systems and become part of the context for subsequent reasoning. Existing studies of post-hallucination reasoning (PHR) mainly characterize changes in final outcomes and aggregate reasoning dynamics, leaving how models resolve hallucinated premises at the response level insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce PHRBench, a controlled benchmark for behaviorally structured PHR across four domains and 18 large language models. PHRBench characterizes each reasoning trajectory independently of final-answer correctness through Hallucination Compliance, Hallucination Avoidance, and Heuristic Correction, and defines an insightful trajectory as successful correction that ultimately reaches the correct answer. Across 4820 controlled instances, we find that successful recovery remains relatively rare and is associated with more frequent belief updates along the reasoning trajectory. We further find that properties of the hallucinated prompt contain substantial predictive signal for successful recovery, with a lightweight predictor achieving an AUROC of 0.847. These findings provide a behavioral view of post-hallucination reasoning, characterizing how LLMs resolve erroneous context and when successful recovery is likely to occur.

</details>


### [219] [Your Prompt Should Do More: Effects of Retrieval Instructions in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10508)
*Amanda Myntti,Jenna Kanerva,Veronika Laippala,Filip Ginter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究检索指令如何影响非对称检索查询表示，发现查询侧干扰项会使模型难以遵循任务指令，并可通过加入干扰项微调来显著缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带指令的嵌入模型在检索中受到关注，但现有模型往往无法可靠遵循检索指令。作者希望揭示指令影响查询表示的机制，并解释模型失效的原因。

Method: 分析指令对非对称检索任务中查询表示的作用机制；在评测中引入查询侧干扰项以考察模型遵循简单任务指令的能力；假设该行为源于当前嵌入模型的训练设置与评测方式；采用加入查询侧干扰项的微调方法进行验证。

Result: 当评测中包含查询侧干扰项时，模型甚至会无法遵循简单任务指令；加入查询侧干扰项进行微调后取得显著改进，且对其他任务影响极小。

Conclusion: 模型对指令的遵循失败与训练及评测设置密切相关，通过在微调中引入查询侧干扰项可有效提升指令遵循能力，同时保持较好的任务泛化性。

Abstract: Prompted embedding models have recently received increasing attention, particularly for retrieval, where detailed retrieval instructions are provided as part of the retrieval prompt. Several new datasets and studies have examined this setting, showing that the current embedding models often struggle to follow such instructions reliably. In this paper, we study the mechanism of how instructions actually affect the representations of retrieval queries in asymmetric retrieval tasks. We show that models can fail to follow even simple task instructions when query-side distractors are included in the evaluation. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by the training setup of current embedding models and their evaluation, and show that fine-tuning with added query-side distractors leads to substantial improvements, with minimal effect on other tasks.

</details>


### [220] [EngramEdit: Decoupled Knowledge Updates in LLMs through Conditional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533)
*Hongru Cai,Ran Wei,Wenjie Wang,Chengfa Wu,Ning Song,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EngramEdit方法，通过条件记忆架构（Engram）实现解耦的事实知识更新，在保持主干网络固定的前提下达到近乎完美的编辑成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆架构（如DeepSeek Engram）利用输入n-gram查表获取嵌入，能以有限额外计算扩展LLM容量，并展现出将事实知识存储与通用计算解耦的潜力，从而有望在固定Transformer主干的情况下更新事实知识。但实现这一潜力存在困难：同一事实的不同表达会激活不同的n-gram嵌入，而更新共享嵌入又可能误改模型对其他事实的预测。

Method: EngramEdit分两步：首先计算目标记忆表示，使模型在多种表达下都能预测更新后的事实；然后联合更新共享n-gram嵌入，使其在多种表达和多次编辑间匹配这些目标，并对被频繁复用的嵌入施加更强的更新惩罚，以保护无关知识。

Result: EngramEdit实现了独立的事实知识更新，编辑成功率近乎完美；修订后的知识可迁移到未见表达并支持多跳推理，在CoT提示下准确率约为最强基线的三倍；即使事实更新不断累积，无关知识与通用能力也基本得以保留。

Conclusion: EngramEdit将条件记忆转变为可编辑的知识接口，使其作用超越模型扩容，可用于支持解耦的知识更新。

Abstract: Conditional memory architectures such as DeepSeek Engram use input n-grams to look up learned embeddings, expanding the capacity of large language models (LLMs) with limited additional computation. Beyond model scaling, this architecture has demonstrated the potential to decouple factual knowledge storage from general-purpose computation, offering a promising route to updating factual knowledge while keeping the Transformer backbone fixed. Realizing this potential is challenging because different expressions of a fact may activate different n-gram embeddings, while updating shared embeddings can unintentionally change the model's predictions about other facts. We propose EngramEdit for decoupled knowledge updates through conditional memory. EngramEdit first computes target memory representations that make the model predict the updated fact across multiple expressions. It then jointly updates the shared n-gram embeddings to match these targets across expressions and edits, penalizing updates to frequently reused embeddings more strongly to preserve unrelated knowledge. Experiments show that EngramEdit enables independent factual knowledge updates through conditional memory, achieving near-perfect editing success. Revised knowledge is usable across unseen expressions and in multi-hop reasoning, with nearly three times the strongest baseline's accuracy under chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting. Unrelated knowledge and general capabilities are largely preserved even as factual updates accumulate. These findings show that EngramEdit turns conditional memory into an editable knowledge interface, extending its role beyond model scaling to support decoupled knowledge updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Adaptive Workflow Intelligence: A Cognitive Architecture for Context-Driven Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08793)
*Sreedevi Pandiyath Viswambaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应工作流智能（AWI）认知架构，以"感知—认知—行动—反思"（PCAR）四层循环组织企业智能体，将反思作为策略持续精炼机制，在延迟反馈与制度漂移的仿真企业流程中验证其稳定—敏捷权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统日益依赖自动化工作流，但多数AI方案在非平稳环境、政策演变与延迟运营反馈下表现脆弱。强化学习与大模型智能体虽具备部分适应性，却缺少持久反思机制，也难以与受政策约束的企业运营直接集成。因此需要一个兼顾反思精炼与合规约束的企业级认知架构。

Method: 提出AWI认知架构，围绕四层感知—认知—行动—反思（PCAR）循环构建情境驱动的企业智能体；将反思设为策略持续精炼机制，并融合混合推理、反思性记忆与反馈驱动适应，以应对环境漂移与运营约束。在具有延迟结果和受控机制突变的仿真企业决策工作流中进行评估，并设置"漂移—延迟"压力测试。

Result: 在漂移—延迟压力测试中，受护栏约束的自适应方法相比静态自动化恢复更快，且保持政策合规；在此设定下，AWI的反思组件适度降低了行为振荡与反馈方差，体现出反思性策略适应带来的稳定性—敏捷性权衡。

Conclusion: 反思机制可在合规约束下提升企业智能体对非平稳环境的适应与恢复能力，但其收益表现为以适度敏捷性换取稳定性的权衡，而非无代价的全面提升。

Abstract: Enterprise systems increasingly rely on automated workflows, yet many AI-driven solutions remain brittle under non-stationary conditions, evolving policies, and delayed operational feedback. While reinforcement learning and large language model (LLM) agents offer partial adaptability, they do not by themselves provide persistent reflection mechanisms or straightforward integration with policy-constrained enterprise operations. This paper introduces Adaptive Workflow Intelligence (AWI), a cognitive architecture for context-driven enterprise agents organized around a four-layer Perception-Cognition-Action-Reflection (PCAR) loop. AWI treats reflection as a mechanism for continuous policy refinement and combines hybrid reasoning with reflective memory and feedback-driven adaptation to support decision making under environmental drift and operational constraints. We evaluate AWI in a simulated enterprise decision workflow characterized by delayed outcomes and a controlled regime shift. In a drift-and-delay stress test, guardrail-constrained adaptive approaches recover more rapidly than static automation while maintaining policy compliance. Within this setting, AWI's reflective components modestly reduce behavioral oscillation and feedback variance, illustrating the stability-agility trade-off introduced by reflective policy adaptation.

</details>


### [222] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GradSAT 将 SMT 子句求解建模为多任务学习任务，通过 GradNorm 动态平衡各子句梯度，并用两阶段 GPU 加速流水线求解 QF_FP 约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的 SMT 求解器在无重量化浮点理论（QF_FP）上受限于梯度支配问题：少数困难子句主导优化轨迹，导致求解器无法满足整体公式并陷入局部极小值。因此需要一种能稳定连续搜索动态、缓解此类脆弱性的方法。

Method: 提出 GradSAT 框架，将约束满足过程重构为多任务学习：把每个 SMT 子句视为独立任务，运行时应用动态梯度归一化（GradNorm）主动平衡各子句梯度幅值，惩罚主导梯度、加速滞后子句以实现均匀收敛。实现上采用两阶段混合流水线：第一阶段用 GPU 加速的 PyTorch 后端结合符号编译与算子融合，在连续松弛上导航至高质量盆地；第二阶段将候选赋值交由位精确（bit-precise）局部搜索引擎，快速求解精确严格的赋值。

Result: 通过稳定连续搜索动态，GradSAT 缓解了先前基于梯度求解器的脆弱性，提供了稳健且高度可并行的复杂约束求解架构。

Conclusion: 将多任务学习中的动态梯度归一化引入基于优化的 SMT 求解，可有效应对梯度支配问题，并结合 GPU 加速连续搜索与位精确局部搜索，形成稳健、可扩展的求解框架。

Abstract: Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solvers are foundational to software verification, program analysis, and compiler testing, particularly over the theory of Quantifier-Free Floating-Point (QF_FP). While recent optimization-based SMT solvers have successfully applied gradient descent to continuous relaxations of logical formulas, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by gradient domination, a phenomenon where a small subset of difficult clauses hijacks the optimization trajectory, preventing the solver from satisfying the broader formula and trapping it in local minima.
  To overcome this, we present GradSAT, a novel framework that bridges optimization-based SMT solving with Multi-Task Learning (MTL). GradSAT reformulates the constraint satisfaction process by treating each SMT clause as an independent MTL task. By applying dynamic gradient normalization (GradNorm), GradSAT actively balances the gradient magnitudes across all clauses at runtime, systematically penalizing dominant gradients and accelerating lagging clauses to ensure uniform convergence. GradSAT implements this through a highly optimized, two-stage hybrid pipeline. First, a GPU-accelerated PyTorch backend leveraging symbolic compilation and operator fusion navigates the continuous relaxation to a high-quality basin. Second, the candidate assignment is handed off to a bit-precise local search engine to rapidly resolve the exact, rigorous assignment. By stabilizing the continuous search dynamics, GradSAT mitigates the brittleness of prior gradient-based solvers and provides a robust, highly parallelizable architecture for complex constraint solving.

</details>


### [223] [Route-Verify-Vote: Procedure-Conditioned Self-Consistency for Mixed-Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08814)
*Xinchen Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Route-Verify-Vote (RVV) 框架，通过领域路由选择推理流程、逐选项验证与答案集投票（含自适应采样分配），在 SCoRE 2026 混合域常识推理任务上达到 79.4% 精确集合准确率，系统排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要以不熟悉方式组合熟悉推理操作时，组合泛化能力仍然不足；SCoRE 2026 在三个未出现在训练中的混合域上考察该能力，且要求模型给出每题的完整正确选项集合，这使传统单一答案生成式自洽方法难以直接适用。

Method: 提出 RVV 框架，属流程条件化的自洽方法，不更新参数：Route 依据给定领域标签选择推理流程，引导模型表示并施加相关约束；Verify 提示模型针对约束逐一评估每个选项；Vote 聚合完整答案集，并对前两名答案集票数差距较小的问题追加采样。每题样本共享同一领域特定流程，并可结合模型集成。

Result: 官方测试集上，每题 16 个采样答案集投票取得 74.6% 精确集合准确率；自适应 RVV 达 77.3%；在选定领域路由上结合多模型提升至 79.4%，最终系统在参赛系统中排名第二。

Conclusion: 领域特定推理流程与答案集分歧可作为混合域推理中分配推理期计算的有效手段。

Abstract: Compositional generalization remains challenging when language models must combine familiar reasoning operations in unfamiliar ways. The Scenario-Based Commonsense Reasoning Evaluation (SCoRE) 2026 tests this ability on three mixed domains absent from training and requires models to identify the complete set of correct options for each question.
  We introduce Route-Verify-Vote (RVV), a framework for procedure-conditioned self-consistency that uses language models without parameter updates. Route uses the provided domain label to select a reasoning procedure that guides the model in representing and applying the relevant constraints. Verify prompts the model to assess each option against those constraints. Vote aggregates complete answer sets and allocates additional samples to questions with a small vote-count margin between the two most frequent sets. Samples for each question follow the same domain-specific procedure.
  On the official test set, voting over 16 sampled answer sets per question achieves an exact-set accuracy of 74.6%. Adaptive RVV reaches 77.3%, and combining models on selected domain routes raises accuracy to 79.4%. The final system ranked second among participating systems. These results support domain-specific reasoning procedures and answer-set disagreement as useful tools for allocating inference-time computation in mixed-domain reasoning.

</details>


### [224] [An Empirical Study of Agent Skills' Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08875)
*Yu Cheng,Dehai Zhao,Zhongxin Liu,Qing Huang,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对87个SkillsBench任务进行实证研究，将Skill下游效用定义为同模型-执行框架下与No-Skill的通过率差，跨9种配置比较同一Skill，发现Skill效用高度依赖配置，并给出17条编写实践与多Skill组织建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多刻画Skill内容或评估下游性能，但缺乏对"效用如何取决于内容、执行配置与多Skill组织"的解释；且存在相关Skill不一定提升任务表现的问题。

Method: 在87个SkillsBench任务上开展实证：以同模型-执行框架下与No-Skill的通过率差定义下游效用；对同一批Skill在9种配置下比较，再在3种选定配置下考察替代的已发布Skill及固定Skill集的不同组织方式；从37,596个Skill的精选语料中检索市场候选；采用LLM辅助分析内容、执行轨迹与最终产物，并经作者复核，将所提供支持与实际使用及任务结果关联。

Result: 同一Skill在36.78%的任务上对部分配置有帮助、对另一些配置有害，轨迹显示推荐流程可能成为执行负担；相关性排序会忽略更有用的候选；按对所需操作的支持度重排后，三种配置下首选通过率提升4.35–5.80个百分点；归纳出17条编写实践，将可执行流程与恢复、任务需求保持及最终产物检查相衔接；Stage Plan与Dependency DAG优于仅用使用顺序，且DAG的额外收益集中在提供五或六个Skill的任务上。

Conclusion: Skill效用取决于内容、执行配置与多Skill组织。建议开发者评估可用操作支持、允许在保持任务需求前提下适配流程，并在组织Skill时显式化产物依赖关系。

Abstract: Agent Skills package procedural guidance and resources for reuse, but a relevant Skill does not necessarily improve task performance. Existing studies characterize Skill content and evaluate downstream performance, yet provide limited explanations of how utility depends on content, execution configuration, and multi-Skill organization. We conduct an empirical study on 87 SkillsBench tasks, defining downstream utility as the pass-rate difference from No-Skill on the same tasks under the same model--harness configuration. We compare the same Skills across nine configurations, then examine alternative published Skills and organizations of fixed Skill sets under three selected configurations. We retrieve marketplace candidates from a curated corpus of 37,596 Skills. LLM-assisted analysis of content, execution traces, and final artifacts, followed by author review, relates provided support to actual use and task outcomes. The same Skills help some configurations and hurt others on 36.78\% of tasks, with trajectories showing that recommended procedures can become an execution burden. Relevance rankings overlook more useful candidates. Within the evaluated candidate sets, reranking by support for required operations raises first-choice pass rates by 4.35--5.80 percentage points across the three configurations. We derive 17 authoring practices linking executable procedures to recovery, preservation of task requirements, and checks on final artifacts. Stage Plan and Dependency DAG outperform use order alone, with DAG's additional benefits concentrated in tasks supplied with five or six Skills. These findings guide developers to assess usable operation support, allow procedure adaptation while preserving task requirements, and make artifact dependencies explicit when organizing Skills.

</details>


### [225] [How Could AI Eliminate Humanity? A Failure-Mode Analysis of Civilizational Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08878)
*Mikołaj Sienicki,Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"失效模式"框架，用于分析先进人工智能如何可能导致人类灭绝、不可逆的文明崩溃或人类的永久性去权，并强调灾难性AI风险并不需要意识、敌意或明确的伤害意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有讨论常将灾难性AI风险归因于AI的意识、敌意或蓄意伤害，这一假设可能过窄，容易遗漏其他同样致命的致灾路径。因此需要一个系统化的失效模式框架，厘清风险产生的多种因果条件。

Method: 采用概念性、非操作化的分析路径：构建失效模式框架，区分若干相互独立又彼此作用的致灾路径（自主性失准、人类有害使用、组织失效、竞争性部署），并识别影响危害严重程度的关键因素（能力、自主性、外部访问、持续性、制度保障、恢复能力保留），以及可实证检验的中间量与防御性研究问题。

Result: 该框架表明，AI灾难性风险来自多条路径及其交互作用，而非单一"恶意AI"情景；危害严重程度取决于能力、自主性、外部访问、持续性、制度保障与恢复能力等中间变量，这些变量为后续实证研究提供了可操作的切入点。

Conclusion: 灾难性AI风险不依赖意识、敌意或伤害意图；通过失效模式视角识别因果条件与中间量，可引导防御性研究，同时该分析刻意保持非操作化以避免提供危害方法。

Abstract: This article develops a failure-mode framework for analyzing how advanced artificial intelligence could contribute to human extinction, irreversible civilizational collapse, or permanent human disempowerment. The central thesis is that catastrophic AI risk does not require consciousness, hostility, or an explicit intention to harm humanity. Instead, risk may arise through several distinct but interacting pathways, including autonomous misalignment, harmful human use, organizational failure, and competitive deployment. The severity of these pathways depends on factors such as capability, autonomy, external access, persistence, institutional safeguards, and the preservation of recovery capacity. The analysis is deliberately non-operational: it identifies causal conditions, empirically tractable intermediate quantities, and defensive research questions rather than procedures for causing harm.

</details>


### [226] [Humanize: Judgement Engineering for Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08900)
*Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu,Zijian Zhang,Dongyun Zou,Zhengyang Zhang,Changye Li,Song Bian,Song Han,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Humanize 是一套面向 agentic coding 的多智能体编排工作流，核心是"判断工程"：由人类批准计划契约、builder 智能体分轮实现、来自不同厂商的 reviewer 智能体判定完成，确定性 hooks（而非模型）负责路由并执行 72 道机械门禁。通过 68 个版本、118 份公开复盘及多项应用验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic coding 让代码生成变得廉价，但可靠"完成"仍困难：写代码的智能体本身就是判断任务是否完成的弱评判者，存在自我评估偏差。

Method: 提出 Humanize 多智能体编排工作流，围绕"判断工程"在规划、实现、审查、学习四个边界设置显式且机械强制的决策。人类批准计划契约；builder 智能体分轮实现；来自另一厂商的 reviewer 智能体判定完成；由确定性 hooks 在角色间路由工作并执行 72 道机械门禁。从马尔可夫链视角看，builder 与 reviewer 交替采样形成两模型联合推断，缺陷唯有双方均漏检才能存活。

Result: 108 天内迭代 68 个版本，获 1,468 GitHub stars；118 份公开复盘显示独立审查能捕获 builder 的无据声明。应用方面：567 文件的 gem5 构建系统迁移（经上游审查）；Kernel Design Agents 在 MLSys 2026 FlashInfer 竞赛全部三个 Full-Agent 赛道均列前三；通过 HOA 在 IOI/IMO/IPhO 2026 与 IBO 2024 取得满分，IChO 2026 得 418.5/437（金牌）；PutnamBench 672/672，Lean-Eval 排行榜第一（251/303）。

Conclusion: 独立审查机制有效，但"停止"仍是关键弱点：按阶段区分轮次的报告中，三分之二的轮次发生在实现被接受之后。该结论为观察性证据，并非对工作流的受控对比。

Abstract: Agentic coding makes code generation cheap, but reliable completion remains difficult: the agent that writes the code is a weak judge of whether it is done.
  We present Humanize, a multi-agent orchestration workflow for agentic coding built around judgement engineering: explicit, mechanically enforced decisions at the boundaries between planning, implementation, review, and learning. A human approves a plan contract, a builder agent implements it in rounds, and a reviewer agent from another vendor decides completion; deterministic hooks, not a model, route work between these roles and enforce 72 mechanical gates. Viewed as a Markov chain over repository states, alternating builder and reviewer samples jointly from two models, so a defect survives only if both miss it. We study Humanize through its deployment, 118 public postmortems of real loops, and its applications. Over 68 versions in 108 days, it gathered 1,468 GitHub stars.
  Applications include a 567-file gem5 build-system migration under upstream review; Kernel Design Agents, which extend the loop with a kernel knowledge base and profiling feedback and placed in the top three of all three Full-Agent tracks of the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer contest; and, through Humanize Olympiad Agents (HOA), full scores in IOI 2026, IMO 2026, IPhO 2026, and IBO 2024, 418.5/437 in IChO 2026 (gold-medal). Humanize also achieves 672/672 on PutnamBench and ranks first (251/303) on Lean-Eval's leaderboard even competiting with professional mathematicians. The postmortems show that independent review catches unsupported builder claims, but stopping remains a key weakness. In reports that separate rounds by phase, two thirds of rounds occurred after implementation was accepted. This evidence is observational, not a controlled comparison of workflows.

</details>


### [227] [Sequential Probabilistic Uncertainty Estimation for Parallel Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08901)
*Tunyu Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yusong Zhao,Haizhou Shi,Zhuohang Li,Haoxian Chen,Hao Wang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAUCE，一种轻量、免训练的不确定性估计器，将并行多智能体推理系统的不确定性建模为对系统级潜在信念的序列推断，在五个骨干模型、五个基准和两种 MAS 协议上提升了误分类检测、选择性预测与校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）通过多智能体交互提升推理能力，其中并行 MAS 让多个智能体多轮求解同一问题并聚合输出。然而，此类系统的不确定性估计仍被忽视：MAS 的可靠性不仅取决于单个生成结果，还取决于智能体跨轮次的交互与演化，因此需要面向系统层面的不确定性度量。

Method: 提出 SAUCE（Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution）。该方法为轻量、免训练的不确定性估计器，将 MAS 不确定性形式化为对潜在系统级信念的序列推断，并通过滤波式（filtering-style）更新，聚合轮级一致性（agreement）与生成不确定性信号。

Result: 在五个骨干模型、五个基准和两种 MAS 协议上，SAUCE 在误分类检测、选择性预测和校准方面均优于广泛的基线，包括标准基于对数似然的方法和 MAS 专用估计器。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性建模为系统级信念的序列推断并融合一致性与生成不确定性，SAUCE 为并行多智能体推理系统提供了有效的免训练不确定性估计方案，验证了从系统交互演化角度刻画 MAS 可靠性的可行性与优势。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have attracted growing attention for improving reasoning through interaction among multiple agents. In this work, we focus on parallel multi-agent reasoning systems, where several agents solve the same problem over multiple rounds and aggregate their outputs into a final answer. Despite their strong reasoning performance, uncertainty estimation for such systems remains underexplored: the reliability of a MAS depends not only on individual generations, but also on how agents interact and evolve across rounds. We propose SAUCE (Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution), a lightweight, training-free uncertainty estimator that formulates MAS uncertainty as sequential inference over a latent system-level belief. SAUCE aggregates round-level agreement and generation-uncertainty signals through a filtering-style update. Across five backbones, five benchmarks, and two MAS protocols, SAUCE improves misclassification detection, selective prediction, and calibration over a broad set of uncertainty estimation baselines, including standard log-likelihood-based methods and MAS-specific estimators.

</details>


### [228] [Agent Plasticity: Measuring Self-Improvement Through Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08902)
*Harman Singh,Anton Bakhtin,Rulin Shao,Gabriel Synnaeve,Ilia Kulikov,Rob Fergus,Sanjeev Arora,Kurt Keutzer,Jason Weston,Anuj Mahajan,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在受控设定下评估AI智能体的自我改进能力，引入"智能体可塑性"（agent plasticity）指标衡量其将经验转化为未来留出性能增益的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多测量智能体在固定时间点的能力，而非其学习效果。评估自我改进需回答三个问题：未来性能是否提升并泛化到促成学习的交互之外；新能力获取的效率如何；自我改进过程在何处失效。

Method: 在受控环境中研究自我改进：智能体将过往经验摊销为可复用产物，并被未来实例继承；在每个检查点测量训练与留出环境交互上的性能，并计入学习成本。据此定义"智能体可塑性"，即智能体将经验转化为未来留出性能增益的效率，并将失败回溯至改进循环各环节。

Result: 在多个环境中，前沿模型尽管学习机会相当，却呈现截然不同的改进轨迹：部分获得显著且持续的增益，另一些则接近或低于初始性能；训练范围内的增益往往仅部分迁移到分布外条件。最终点能力与获取效率出现背离——最终表现最佳者未必是改进效率最高者。

Conclusion: 低可塑性智能体常无法复用相关产物，而较高可塑性者即便复用了相关产物仍可能失败，指向产物质量、泛化或应用方面的局限。评估自我改进智能体不仅要衡量其能做什么，还需衡量其通过经验变得更好的效率。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly operate in environments where they can diagnose failures and improve through experience, yet existing evaluations largely measure what an agent can do at a fixed point in time rather than how effectively it learns. Evaluating self-improvement requires answering three questions: does future performance improve and generalize beyond the interactions that enabled learning; how efficiently are new capabilities acquired; and where does the self-improvement process break down? To answer these questions, we study self-improvement in a controlled setting where agents amortize past experience into reusable artifacts that are inherited by future instances. At each checkpoint, we measure performance on training and held-out environment interactions while accounting for learning cost. We introduce agent plasticity, the efficiency with which an agent converts experience into gains in future held-out performance. Across multiple environments, frontier models exhibit sharply different improvement trajectories despite comparable opportunities to learn. Some achieve substantial and persistent gains, while others remain near or below their initial performance, and gains within the training regime often transfer only partially to out-of-distribution conditions. Endpoint capability and acquisition efficiency also diverge: the agent that ultimately performs best need not be the one that improves most efficiently. Tracing failures through the improvement loop further reveals different candidate bottlenecks. Agents with low plasticity often fail to reuse relevant artifacts, whereas more plastic agents may still fail despite reusing relevant artifacts, pointing to limitations in artifact quality, generalization, or application. Evaluating self-improving agents requires measuring not only what they can do, but how effectively they become better through experience.

</details>


### [229] [AdaGuard: Enhancing Safety and Policy Compliance with Reasoning-Enabled LLM-As-A-Judge Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08923)
*Melissa Kazemi Rad,Sihui Dai,Isha Slavin,Kushal Chawla,Mann Patel,Jian Ni,William M. Campbell,Stephen Rawls,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AdaGuard——一种自适应 LLM-as-a-Judge 安全护栏框架，通过动态策略执行与自适应推理预算分配，在运行时泛化到用户自定义策略，兼顾低延迟与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级生成式 AI 应用需要适应多种风险姿态、不断演变的政策与不同延迟约束的安全机制。现有护栏方案僵化，依赖固定策略集，透明度与推理灵活性不足。

Method: 基于监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（GRPO）构建；核心创新是动态推断输入-策略对的复杂度，从而在高速度黑盒推理与可解释的推理式审核之间切换。

Result: AdaGuard 可媲美参数规模数倍于自身的其他护栏模型与前沿模型；其自动推理模式在极低延迟下恢复了常开推理的准确率。

Conclusion: 自适应推理预算分配使护栏系统能够在严格延迟要求与可操作透明度之间取得平衡，为动态策略执行提供可行路径。

Abstract: Enterprise generative AI applications require robust safety mechanisms that can accommodate diverse risk postures, evolving policies, and varying latency constraints. Current guardrail solutions often suffer from rigidity, relying on fixed policy sets and offering limited transparency or reasoning flexibility. We present Adaguard, an adaptive LLM-as-a-Judge framework designed to address these challenges through dynamic policy enforcement and adaptive reasoning-budget allocation. Built using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (GRPO), AdaGuard generalizes to user-defined safety and compliance policies at runtime without requiring frequent model updates. A core innovation of our approach is the ability to dynamically infer the complexity of input-policy pairs, allowing the model to switch between high-speed black-box inference and explainable, reasoning-enabled moderation. This flexibility enables developers to balance stringent latency requirements with the need for actionable transparency. This adaptive capability allows AdaGuard to rival other guardrail and frontier models several times its size, while its auto-reasoning mode recovers the accuracy of always-on reasoning at a fraction of the latency

</details>


### [230] [Can AI Agents Make Open-Ended Scientific Discovery? Evidence from Station](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08927)
*Wenyu Du,Stephen Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在开放世界科学发现环境 Station 中引入 Supervisor 机制与周期性 Meta Reflection，使智能体在缺乏中间指标时仍能持续探索；在三个 ICLR 论文衍生的开放式任务上平均重新发现 62.7% 的原始发现条目，显著优于 Codex Multiagent-v2（15.4%）与 AI Scientist-v2（14.4–20.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 系统在指标明确的科学发现任务上进展迅速，但能否自主开展开放式科学发现尚不明确；开放式任务缺乏中间指标与反馈信号，导致探索难以持续。

Method: 构建开放世界多智能体模拟科学生态的 Station 环境，并为其增加两个机制：Supervisor 机制与周期性 Meta Reflection；从三篇 ICLR 口头论文构建开放式任务，仅提供研究主问题、隐藏结果并禁用网络访问；将原始发现拆分为独立条目，统计智能体重新发现的比例，并进行消融与行为分析，另在两项无参考答案的开放式任务上评估。

Result: Station 平均重新发现 62.7% 的条目，Codex Multiagent-v2 为 15.4%，AI Scientist-v2 为 14.4–20.6%；消融与行为分析表明两机制结合可提升研究覆盖度与连续性；在无参考答案任务中，部分发现与知识截止日期之后研究者报告的发现高度吻合。

Conclusion: 合适的开放世界环境可使智能体在开放式科学发现中自主取得有意义进展，环境设计与探索机制是关键因素。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have made rapid progress in scientific discovery when given well-defined metrics, but whether they can autonomously undertake open-ended scientific discovery remains unclear. We investigate AI's ability to tackle open-ended tasks in Station, an open-world environment in which multiple agents simulate a scientific ecosystem. To tackle challenges specific to open-ended tasks, we propose augmenting Station with two mechanisms: a Supervisor mechanism and periodic Meta Reflection, which encourage persistent exploration even when intermediate metrics are lacking. We construct open-ended tasks from three recent oral papers presented at ICLR. We give agents the main research question studied in each paper while withholding the paper's results and disabling web access. We then measure how many of the original findings-partitioned into individual criteria-agents rediscover. We find that Station rediscovers 62.7% of the criteria on average, compared with 15.4% for Codex Multiagent-v2 and 14.4-20.6% for AI Scientist-v2. Ablation and behavioral analyses indicate that adding the two mechanisms together improves research coverage and continuity. We further evaluate Station on two open-ended tasks without oracle papers and find that some of the discoveries made by the agents closely match discoveries reported by researchers after the knowledge cutoff date. Together, these results indicate that a suitable environment can enable agents to autonomously make meaningful progress in open-ended scientific discovery.

</details>


### [231] [Humanity's Sixth Sense: Benchmarking Intuitive Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08966)
*Xingang Guo,Jing Gu,Brian Jang,Renxiong Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Daniel Quigley,Steven Li,David Yan,Daniel Yue Zhang,Darvin Yi,Forrest Huang,HiJae Kim,Tianyi Zhang,Jared Lichtarge,Jihua Huang,Le Xue,Manan Tomar,Qiuyi Richard Zhang,Ruofei Yu,Seth Neel,Yaning Hu,Marcella Valentine,Xinzhe Jiang,Daniel Evans,Chenguang Wang,Dustin Tran,Tong Zhao,Yinfei Yang,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HSS（Humanity's Sixth Sense）基准，用于评测多模态大模型在图像与视频上的直觉视觉推理能力，即从一瞥中恢复隐含的时间、空间、社会与抽象信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能凭一瞥推断过去成因与未来轨迹、判断车辆能否停入车位、识别房间中的权威关系、捕捉不成文规则等隐含信息，这种零样本的快速视觉直觉支撑日常导航与社会交互，是部署于人类身边的 MLLM 的关键能力。然而现有视觉基准要么面向学术与数学领域的专家级分析，要么局限于低层感知，导致这种直觉推理能力基本未被评测。

Method: 构建 HSS 基准：覆盖多样化的图像与视频输入；以结构化分类法组织测试项；每项配备人工撰写的提示词，用于探测人类一眼即可推断的隐含时间、空间、社会与抽象结构。此外还探索了在 HSS 上应用动态视觉操控的智能体（agentic）设置。

Result: 前沿 MLLM 显著落后于人类：人类参与者准确率 93.1%，而最强模型 GPT-6-astra 即使在最大推理投入下也仅 53.6%。智能体设置可缩小差距但无法弥合。

Conclusion: 当前模型虽在需要高级感知与知识的复杂任务上表现出色，却在人类凭直觉即可完成的视觉任务上仍存在重大短板。HSS 将直觉视觉推理确立为可测量的能力维度，指向一个在当前基准上扩大规模所未能覆盖的能力方向。

Abstract: Humans perceive far more in a scene than what is explicitly depicted: a single glance captures past causes and future trajectories; a quick peek determines if a vehicle can fit between two parked cars; a few seconds of video reveals who holds authority in a room; and a fleeting clip highlights subtle abstract patterns like unwritten rules or hidden labels. This capacity reflects a form of humanity's sixth sense: an intuitive reasoning mechanism that recovers implicit information beyond raw sensory perception. Crucially, this rapid, zero-shot visual intuition underpins everyday navigation and social interaction, making it a vital capability for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) deployed alongside people. Existing visual benchmarks, however, target either deliberate expert-level analysis in academic and mathematical domains or low-level perception, leaving the intuitive reasoning that people perform largely untested. To bridge this gap, we introduce Humanity's Sixth Sense (HSS), a benchmark for intuitive visual reasoning. HSS spans diverse image and video inputs, organizes items under a structured taxonomy, and pairs each with human-written prompts probing the implicit temporal, spatial, social, and abstract structure that people infer at a glance. Frontier MLLMs fall short of human performance: participants reach 93.1% accuracy, while the strongest model, GPT-6-astra, reaches only 53.6% even at maximum reasoning effort. Despite excelling in many complex tasks that require advanced perception and knowledge, current models still struggle significantly on these visual tasks that are intuitive for humans. We further explore agentic setup that apply dynamic visual manipulation to HSS, which narrows but does not close the gap. HSS establishes intuitive visual reasoning as a measurable axis and directs attention to a capability that scaling on current benchmarks has so far left behind.

</details>


### [232] [Socio-Foundation: A Model for Generalizable Individual Behavior Simulation via Hierarchical Capability Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08967)
*Liang Wang,Wenxuan Xie,Xinyi Mou,Yixin Luo,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出FONTS分类体系（人格保真、结果实现、行为自然性、轨迹连贯性、社会根基），构建约1000万条实例的标准化语料库，并给出Socio-Foundation三阶段训练管线与IndiEval评测基准，使模型在个体行为模拟上显著超越Qwen3-8B基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟个体行为要求大语言模型在保持人格特质的同时适应动态社会语境，但通用LLM往往会抹平不同人格的差异，而任务专用微调则存在碎片化与泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 将个体模拟组织为FONTS五维能力分类体系；据此构建约1000万实例、覆盖14个数据集的标准化训练语料库；提出Socio-Foundation三阶段管线：先用DAPO学习任务专家，再通过异策略蒸馏整合为能力专家，最后用多教师同策略蒸馏（MOPD）统一；并建立涵盖29项指标的IndiEval评测。

Result: Socio-Foundation较Qwen3-8B基线提升11.0分，接近GLM-5.2等前沿模型；消融实验与分布外评测进一步验证了其有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过分类体系驱动的数据组织与分阶段蒸馏训练，可在统一模型中兼顾人格保真与语境适应，为个体行为模拟提供可扩展、可评测的基础方案。

Abstract: Simulating individual behavior requires large language models (LLMs) to preserve persona traits while adapting to dynamic social contexts. However, general-purpose LLMs often flatten distinct personas, while task-specific tuning suffers from fragmentation and generalization. To overcome these challenges, we organize individual simulation into the \textbf{FONTS Taxonomy}, comprising five complementary capability dimensions: \emph{persona fidelity} (\textbf{F}), \emph{outcome realization} (\textbf{O}), \emph{behavioral naturalness} (\textbf{N}), \emph{trajectory coherence} (\textbf{T}), and \emph{social grounding} (\textbf{S}). Grounded in this taxonomy, we curate a standardized training corpus library of approximately 10 million instances across 14 representative datasets and present \textbf{Socio-Foundation}. Socio-Foundation decouples specialization from integration via a three-stage pipeline: learning task experts via DAPO, consolidating them into capability experts via off-policy distillation, and unifying them via multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). We also establish \textbf{IndiEval}, consolidating 29 metrics across the FONTS dimensions. Experiments show that Socio-Foundation outperforms its \textit{Qwen3-8B} base by 11.0 points and approaches frontier models such as \textit{GLM-5.2}, with ablations and out-of-distribution evaluations further demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization of our model.

</details>


### [233] [Verify Less, Evolve More: Training Idea-Level Critics for Verification-Efficient ML Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08993)
*Jiamu Bai,Lizhu Zhang,Xin Yu,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang,Serena Li,Weiwei Li,Zhuokai Zhao,Lingzhou Xue,Kiwan Maeng,Xiangjun Fan,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI4ML中验证成本高昂、限制自进化智能体效率的问题，提出专门的想法级评审模型（idea-level critic），预测ML修改方案是否优于当前解，从而筛选想法、集中验证资源。评审模型经Gemini-3.1-Pro合成高质量评审的监督微调，再用GRPO进一步优化。实验表明其静态评估优于Gemini-3.1-Pro，并在推理进化、持续学习与策略训练中带来提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型能力增强，自进化智能体可处理AI4ML等复杂任务，但经验验证（模型训练与评估）计算开销大，制约了智能体进化的速度和规模。验证效率此前未被充分探索，且前沿模型直接用作想法选择器时收益有限，因此需要专门的想法级评审模型来提升验证资源的利用效率。

Method: 训练专门的想法级评审模型，预测某个ML修改能否改进当前方案，使智能体能够筛选想法并把验证资源集中于最有潜力的候选。训练方式为两阶段：首先在Gemini-3.1-Pro合成的高质量评审上进行监督微调，然后使用GRPO进一步提升预测准确性。

Result: 评审模型在静态想法评估上超过Gemini-3.1-Pro，且该优势可迁移到智能体推理、持续学习和策略训练。在推理时进化中，相同验证预算下通过选出更优想法提升最终解质量，持续学习可带来进一步增益；在策略训练中，评审模型作为学习到的奖励模型，将经验验证保留给不确定样本，从而在相同验证资源下实现显著更多的策略更新。

Conclusion: 想法级评审模型能在有限验证预算下帮助ML智能体发现更优解并学习更强的提案策略，说明专门化的验证效率优化是提升自进化智能体能力的有效途径。

Abstract: As large language models become more powerful, self-evolving agents are able to tackle challenging tasks including AI for machine learning (AI4ML). In AI4ML, while empirical verification is available, it often requires computationally costly model training and evaluation, limiting the speed and scale of agent evolution. Yet verification efficiency remains under-explored, and frontier models provide only limited gains when used directly as idea selectors. We address this gap with specialized idea-level critic models that predict whether a proposed ML modification will improve upon the current solution, allowing agents to screen ideas and concentrate verification resources on the most promising candidates. We train the critic models through supervised fine-tuning on high-quality critiques synthesized by Gemini-3.1-Pro, followed by GRPO to further improve their predictive accuracy. Empirically, our critic models outperform Gemini-3.1-Pro in static idea evaluation, and these gains extend to agent inference, continual learning, and policy training. During inference-time evolution, they improve final solution quality under the same verification budget by selecting more promising ideas, with further gains from continual learning. During policy training, they serve as learned reward models, reserving empirical verification for uncertain cases and enabling substantially more policy updates with the same verification resources. Together, these results show that idea-level critic models help ML agents discover better solutions and learn stronger proposal policies under limited verification budgets.

</details>


### [234] [How Fragile Is On-Device Language Model Safety? Localizing Safety-Critical Parameters for Sparse Fault Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09000)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLaMA-2-7B-Chat的安全行为集中在极稀疏的参数子集中，仅改动down_proj中0.19%的权重即可使攻击成功率显著上升而通用性能基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型越来越多被部署在资源受限和端侧平台，并作为智能体系统的组件，本地存储的模型参数完整性因而成为重要的安全关切。

Method: 采用两种互补的定位方法：低秩安全相关子空间分析（low-rank safety-associated subspace analysis）与参数级安全—效用重要性过滤（parameter-level safety–utility importance filtering）。

Result: 网络中的安全敏感性高度非均匀分布，MLP的down_proj始终是最突出的安全敏感组件，o_proj贡献较小。基于参数级定位，仅修改down_proj中0.19%的权重即可达到53% Basic ASR和56% GCG ASR，而tinyBenchmarks准确率为51.6%，未修改基线为52.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明确有必要针对资源受限、端侧与智能体部署场景下的语言模型开展定向故障分析与选择性完整性保护。

Abstract: As small language models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed on resource-constrained and on-device platforms, including as components of agentic systems, the integrity of locally stored model parameters becomes an important safety concern. We investigate whether safety-sensitive behavior in LLaMA-2-7B-Chat is concentrated within a sparse subset of parameters, creating a reduced fault surface for targeted analysis. We study two complementary localization methods: low-rank safety-associated subspace analysis and parameter-level safety--utility importance filtering. Both approaches reveal highly non-uniform safety sensitivity across the network, with the MLP down_proj consistently emerging as a prominent safety-sensitive component and o_proj providing a smaller contribution. Using parameter-level localization, modifying only 0.19% of model weights in down_proj yields 53% Basic ASR and 56% GCG ASR, while tinyBenchmarks accuracy remains at 51.6% compared with a 52.2% unmodified baseline. These results motivate targeted fault analysis and selective integrity protection for language models deployed in resource-constrained, on-device, and agentic settings.

</details>


### [235] [Learning to Report Unsafe Tasks in a Multi-Agent Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09002)
*Avyay M. Casheekar,Hariganesh Tangirala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多见证者共享策略下,通过审计使"报告不安全作业"成为最优策略的学习问题,给出精确策略梯度收敛的审计条件,并在PPO实验中验证分离共享策略可显著减少不安全完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多个见证者共享完成任务所获奖励时,报告不安全作业会因中止任务而降低报告者自身收益,因此理性的智能体倾向于沉默。审计虽能使报告变得最优,但并不能保证进一步训练能让"沉默团队"学会报告。论文旨在从理论与实验两方面刻画这一学习难题的激励结构与可学习性条件。

Method: 将问题建模为任一见证者均可通过报告中止任务的博弈:每个任务含k个见证者,共享同一策略且独立抽样。分析在全体沉默处期望奖励对共享沉默概率的导数——每项任务收益被计k次,而全体报告时仅计1次。对任意策略分组,给出足以使精确策略梯度更新收敛到全体报告的审计条件,并证明其在接近全体沉默时(除边界情形)亦为必要条件。在平衡策略族中计算满足条件且具规定正边际的最省审计成本,比较完全共享与每角色单策略的代价。随后用PPO在24个见证者图上从学会沉默的状态开始训练。

Result: 平衡策略族中最省审计成本:完全共享恰为每角色单策略的k倍。PPO实验中,分离共同见证者相比同规模打乱分组,在相同审计下将不安全完成率降低33.59个百分点(95%图自助区间21.03–45.13)。48次见证组运行中仅9次达到不安全完成率低于1%且合法完成率至少90%。相同审计预算下,完全共享网络在独立动作抽样时0/48达标,而在共同抽样时48/48达标。

Conclusion: 共享策略带来"搭便车/沉默"的梯度放大问题:全体沉默处的奖励导数对每任务收益计k次,导致收敛到报告所需的审计成本随共享程度线性增长,而策略耦合又增加学习难度。分离见证者(每角色独立策略)可以更低的审计代价实现更可靠的安全报告行为,但完全共享网络配合动作抽样方式(共同抽样)时表现截然不同,说明策略共享结构与动作相关性是决定审计有效性及安全学习成败的关键因素。

Abstract: When agents share a reward for completed tasks, reporting unsafe work can reduce the reporter's reward by stopping a task. Audits can make reporting optimal without ensuring that further training teaches a silent team to report. We study this learning problem in a game where any witness can stop a task by reporting. With $k$ witnesses per task sharing a policy and drawing independently, the expected-reward derivative with respect to their shared silence probability counts each task's benefit $k$ times at universal silence. The comparison with universal reporting counts it once. For arbitrary policy groups, we give an audit condition sufficient for exact policy-gradient updates to reach universal reporting and, apart from boundary cases, necessary near universal silence. In a balanced family, the cheapest audits meeting the condition with prescribed positive margins cost exactly $k$ times as much for full sharing as for one policy per role. We train PPO policies on 24 witness graphs from learned silence. Separating co-witnesses reduces unsafe completion by 33.59 percentage points compared with shuffled groups of the same sizes under the same audits (95% graph-bootstrap interval: 21.03-45.13). Only 9 of 48 witness-group runs achieve below 1% unsafe completion while retaining at least 90% legitimate completion. At the same audit budget, a fully shared network meets both thresholds in none of 48 runs with independent action draws and all 48 with a common draw.

</details>


### [236] [Sigma-Hunter: A Domain-Specific Language Model for Threat Hunting and Detection Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09007)
*Kemal Davaslioglu,Sastry Kompella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sigma-Hunter，一种面向检测工程领域的适配大语言模型，用于辅助分析师生成 Sigma 规则并进行威胁狩猎。基于 3,635 条经过验证的开源 Sigma 规则构建指令微调数据集（扩展为 7,663 条问答与推理样本），采用 LoRA 微调 7B Mistral 与 Phi-4 模型。Sigma-Hunter-Mistral 综合得分 8.17，优于最强通用基线（7.88）和未微调 Mistral（4.61）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测工程师需将威胁报告、取证观察和狩猎假设转化为精确、可测试的规则。通用大语言模型虽能起草此类规则，但常产生无效 YAML、错误日志源、不受支持字段或过宽的检测逻辑，难以满足检测工程对准确性和语义质量的要求。

Method: 构建来自 3,635 条已验证开源 Sigma 规则的指令微调数据集，扩展为 7,663 条问答与分析师推理样本；在扩展前将每条源规则划分至单一训练/验证/测试分区以避免数据泄漏。使用 LoRA 对 7B Mistral 和 Phi-4 模型进行微调，并对留出规则生成从语法、近似字段一致性以及检测逻辑、完整性、选择性、日志源对齐等语义维度进行评估。

Result: Sigma-Hunter-Mistral 综合得分 8.17，最强通用基线为 7.88，未微调 Mistral 为 4.61。两项发现突出：领域适配使紧凑的 7B 模型在该结构化任务上可与更大通用模型竞争；语法有效性是语义规则质量的弱代理，多个基线虽输出格式良好的 YAML 却带有较弱的检测逻辑。适配模型可本地运行，适用于无法访问托管模型服务的隔离环境。

Conclusion: 领域适配能够使小型（7B）模型在 Sigma 规则生成这一结构化检测工程任务上达到与更大通用模型相当的性能，且本地部署适配模型更适合隔离的检测工程环境。同时，语法有效性不足以反映语义检测质量，评估需重视语义维度。

Abstract: Detection engineers must translate threat reports, forensic observations, and hunt hypotheses into precise, testable rules. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) can draft such rules, but often produce invalid YAML, incorrect log sources, unsupported fields, or overly broad detection logic. This paper presents \emph{Sigma-Hunter}, a domain-adapted LLM for analyst-assistive Sigma rule generation and threat hunting. We build an instruction-tuning dataset from 3,635 validated open-source Sigma rules, expanded into 7,663 question-answer and analyst-reasoning examples. Each source rule is assigned to a single train, validation, or test partition before this expansion, so no rule leaks across splits. We fine-tune a 7B Mistral model and a Phi-4 model with LoRA and score held-out rule generations on syntax, approximate field consistency, and a semantic judgment of detection logic, completeness, selectivity, and log-source alignment. Sigma-Hunter-Mistral scores 8.17 overall, against 7.88 for the strongest general-purpose baseline and 4.61 for untuned Mistral. Two findings stand out: domain adaptation enables a compact 7B model to perform competitively with larger general-purpose models on this structured task, and syntactic validity is a weak proxy for semantic rule quality, as several baselines emit well-formed YAML carrying weak detection logic. The adapted models run locally, which suits detection engineering in disconnected environments where analysts cannot reach hosted model services.

</details>


### [237] [Whose Memory Is It? Scope-Aware Commit Rules for Long-Term LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09008)
*Hongyu Gu,Xinchang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"话语归属"（discourse ownership）概念，并设计CASK提交规则，在持久记忆写入时保留命题的归属上下文，从而避免临时性推理内容污染后续答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆使LLM智能体能够跨对话积累经验，但同时会把局部推理错误固化为长期事实。在推理过程中，智能体可能考虑某计划、模拟工具结果或转述他人信念，随后又将其否决；若记忆仅保留最终句子，这些一度有用的可能性日后会被误当作事实返回。问题根源在于记录被剥离了其有效性的语境。

Method: 1) 将缺失的语境定义为"话语归属"：即许可某命题的世界、分支或说话者。2) 发现语言模型内部已存在因果活跃的归属信号，但常规记忆接口在把推理转成记录时会丢弃该信号。3) 提出CASK（因果锚定作用域键），一种保留该信号的提交规则：共享世界的事实进入持久记忆，暂定内容仅在其原始作用域内可用。4) 指出直接存储内部坐标不可靠（等价表示未必共享坐标），因此CASK改为保留表达归属的稳定关系。

Result: 在受控的长对话冲突与工具智能体轨迹实验中，该设计改善了记忆准入，防止暂定内容污染后续回答，并与运行时溯源机制形成互补。

Conclusion: 该提交边界让智能体能够探索更多可能性，而无需让每句中间语句都获得对未来行为的权威性。

Abstract: Persistent memory allows an LLM agent to carry experience across conversations, but it also turns a local reasoning mistake into a durable one. During deliberation, an agent may consider a plan, simulate a tool result, report another speaker's belief, and then reject all of them. If memory retains only the resulting sentences, those once-useful possibilities can later return as facts. The record is neither fabricated nor irrelevant; it has simply been detached from the context in which it was valid. We identify this missing context as \emph{discourse ownership}: the world, branch, or speaker that licenses a proposition. Our first finding is counterintuitive. Language models already carry a causally active signal for ownership, yet conventional memory interfaces discard it when they convert reasoning into records. We introduce CASK (Causally Anchored Scoping Keys), a commit rule that preserves this signal so that shared-world facts enter durable memory while provisional content remains available only within its original scope. Our second finding is that the most obvious way to preserve the signal---storing the discovered internal coordinates---is unreliable because equivalent representations need not keep the same coordinates. CASK instead preserves the stable relations that express ownership. Controlled long-conversation conflicts and tool-agent traces show that this design improves memory admission and prevents provisional content from contaminating later answers while complementing runtime provenance. The resulting commit boundary lets agents explore more possibilities without granting every intermediate sentence authority over future behavior.

</details>


### [238] [Enabling Dynamic Computation in Looped LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09013)
*Aayush Mishra,Arnau Padrés Masdemont,Victor Conchello Vendrell,Jordi Ros Giralt,Arash Behboodi,Fabio Valerio Massoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环语言模型（Looped LMs）动态计算未真正落地的问题，提出"最优可用"KV缓存策略与早退先验修正，实现最高30%的FLOPs与KV内存削减且保持全深度性能，并提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环LM号称参数高效并支持动态计算（在简单token上节省内存与FLOPs），但当前先进的开源循环LM（Ouro）实际无法兑现：每次循环迭代（深度）都需独立KV缓存，导致必须完成全部循环计算；此外Ouro的早退先验对所有token一视同仁，使所有token无论难易都按较低的等效深度处理。

Method: 1) 提出即插即用的"最优可用"（best-available）KV缓存策略，在性能与深度之间开辟新的前沿；2) 让循环LM在训练时感知该KV缓存策略；3) 对早退先验的约束目标做小幅但有效的修正，使token依据所需计算量在真正异质的深度退出。

Result: 在保持全深度性能的前提下，FLOPs与KV内存最多减少30%；训练时引入KV缓存感知可同时提升性能与效率；早退修正使token退出深度呈现真正的异质性。结论在Ouro模型及从零预训练的较小循环LM上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作释放了循环LM动态计算的真实灵活性，通过KV缓存策略与早退目标修正，在性能—深度权衡上取得新的帕累托改进，并可推广至不同规模的循环LM。

Abstract: Looped LMs are parameter efficient and promise dynamic computation (saving memory and FLOPs on easy tokens). However, state-of-the-art open Looped LMs trained with this dynamic computation capability (Ouro models) do not realize it in practice as each loop iteration (depth) requires its own level of KV-cache, necessitating all loop computations. Moreover, Ouro's early-exit prior is enforced on each token equally, which results in static lower-depth like processing of all tokens regardless of difficulty. In this work, we propose a simple "best-available" KV caching strategy that works out-of-the-box, creating a new frontier in the performance vs depth space. Our approach enables up to 30% reduction in FLOPs and KV memory while retaining full-depth performance, showing the true flexibility of Looped LMs. Furthermore, training looped LMs with awareness about this KV caching strategy improves performance and efficiency. Finally, we apply a small but effective fix to the early-exit prior enforcement objective that makes tokens exit at truly heterogeneous depths based on effort. Our findings are validated on Ouro models as well as smaller looped LMs pre-trained from scratch.

</details>


### [239] [PAIR: Bridging Perception and Action in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09016)
*Kaixi Feng,Guoheng Sun,Ang li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAIR框架，通过在训练时用掩码动作自编码器生成动作隐令牌并与视觉-语言特征对齐，构建共享的感知-动作中间表征，提升连续动作VLA模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作VLA需从描述场景与指令的表征过渡到支持动作生成的表征，但现有方法多依赖最终动作预测损失隐式监督该过渡，缺乏显式的共享中间表征。

Method: 训练阶段，Masked Action Autoencoder将专家动作块编码为与预测视界对齐的Action Latent Tokens；Bridge Module从视觉-语言表征中提取任务相关特征，并将其与Action Latent Tokens对齐形成Bridge Tokens，再投影到动作令牌空间并注入初始Action Tokens，作为Action Expert精炼的起点。推理阶段移除自编码器，Bridge Tokens仅由当前观测与指令生成。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus与CALVIN ABC-D上均取得提升：LIBERO-Plus上VLA-Adapter成功率由59.1%升至64.2%；CALVIN上平均完成序列长度由4.42升至4.53；七个真实任务上OpenVLA-OFT成功率由51.4%升至65.0%。表征分析表明Bridge Tokens在保留任务信息的同时使连续动作信息在Action Expert精炼前可访问。

Conclusion: 共享的中间表征可作为连续动作VLA中感知与动作之间的有效接口，支持更好的动作生成。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions to continuous robot actions. This task requires a transition from representations that describe the scene and instruction to representations that support action generation. Many continuous-action VLAs leave this transition implicit and supervise it mainly through the final action-prediction loss. We introduce PAIR, a framework that learns a shared perception-action representation between these two spaces. During training, a Masked Action Autoencoder encodes expert action chunks into horizon-aligned Action Latent Tokens. A Bridge Module extracts task-relevant features from the current visual-language representations. PAIR aligns these features with the Action Latent Tokens to form Bridge Tokens that preserve task information and capture the structure of expert actions. The Bridge Tokens are then projected into the action-token space and injected into the initial Action Tokens, providing an action-ready starting point for Action Expert refinement. At inference, the autoencoder is removed, and the Bridge Tokens are generated only from the current observation and instruction. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and CALVIN ABC-D show gains for the evaluated OpenVLA-OFT and VLA-Adapter models. On LIBERO-Plus, PAIR raises VLA-Adapter's success rate from 59.1% to 64.2%. On CALVIN, it increases VLA-Adapter's average completed sequence length from 4.42 to 4.53. Across seven real-world tasks, PAIR raises OpenVLA-OFT's success rate from 51.4% to 65.0%. Representation analyses show that Bridge Tokens retain task information while making continuous-action information accessible before Action Expert refinement. These results support a shared intermediate representation as a useful interface between perception and action in continuous-action VLAs.

</details>


### [240] [Not Every Call Needs a Frontier Model: Per-Call-Site Evaluation of Small Language Models in a Deployed Agentic Home-Automation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09021)
*Panagiotis Kasnesis,Christos Chatzigeorgiou,Lazaros Toumanidis,Amalia Contiero Syropoulou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在已部署开源家庭自动化框架 Wactorz 的五个 LLM 调用点上评测 9 个模型（0.8B 至前沿托管模型），用未修改的生产提示和两个真实 Home Assistant 安装（280 用例、2520 次打分调用）。发现能力在不同调用点上排序不一致、更大模型并非一致更优，且仅代码生成能区分本地与托管模型；按调用点路由到最佳本地模型可达 91.8%（最优统一方案 95.4%）且无单次调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统包含多种结构不同的 LLM 调用点（意图路由、动作分类、设备注册表上的语言落地、多智能体流水线规划、生成流水线运行的 Python 代码），难度相差一个数量级。但实践中往往只用为最难调用点挑选的单一模型服务所有调用点，造成成本与能力错配。作者希望验证：同一模型是否在所有调用点上均最优，以及能否按调用点选择更小、更便宜的本地模型。

Method: 在 Wactorz 五个调用点上，用其未修改的生产提示，对 9 个模型（0.8B 至前沿托管模型）在 280 个用例、共 2520 次打分调用上进行评测；使用配对检验比较本地最佳模型与两个托管模型；并做聚合准确率之外的行动（actuation）安全性分析，以及一次真实部署的用户评判。

Result: 能力在不同调用点上排序不同，更大模型并非一致更优：某 4B 模型在落地行动上反而不如其 2B 同类。配对检验显示最佳本地模型在五个调用点中的四个上与两个托管模型统计不可区分；仅代码生成能区分（对小托管模型 p=0.039，对前沿模型 p=0.002）。聚合准确率掩盖了行动特有的安全失败：小模型以退化方式处理准确率/拒答权衡，例如 Gemma4 E2B 在 87.2% 的“设备不归本系统所有”的请求上仍执行行动，而另一模型对所有请求一律拒绝。按调用点路由到最佳本地模型达 91.8%，统一方案为 95.4%，且无单次调用成本。真实部署中仅托管两个生成式调用点即可与全托管持平（39/43 对 39/43），花费仅为 28%；基准预测的行动差距在 26 例中仅出现 1 例。

Conclusion: 单一“最难调用点”模型服务全部调用点并非最优；能力在不同调用点上排序不一致，应按调用点做模型路由。仅托管生成式调用点即可在真实部署中达到全托管效果，显著降低成本。聚合准确率会掩盖行动安全失败，需按调用点分别评估；基准预测的行动差距在真实部署中影响有限。基准、评测框架与全部记录已开源。

Abstract: An agentic system issues several structurally different kinds of LLM calls. It routes intent, classifies actions, grounds language in a device registry, plans multi-agent pipelines and writes the Python code those pipelines run. The difficulty of these call sites varies by an order of magnitude, yet in practice a single model, chosen for the hardest site, serves all of them. In this work, we evaluate 9 models from 0.8B to a frontier hosted model across the five call sites of a deployed open-source home-automation framework (Wactorz), using its unmodified production prompts and two real Home Assistant installations (280 cases, 2520 scored calls). We find that capability is not ordered the same way at every site, and that larger models are not uniformly better: one 4B model is worse than its 2B sibling at grounded actuation. Paired testing shows the best local model to be statistically indistinguishable from both hosted models at four of five sites. Only code generation separates them, against a small hosted model (p = 0.039) as well as a frontier one (p = 0.002). Aggregate accuracy also hides a safety failure specific to actuation, where small models resolve the accuracy/refusal trade-off in degenerate ways: one model (Gemma4 E2B) actuates on 87.2% of requests for devices the site does not own, while another refuses every request it receives. Routing each site to its best local model reaches 91.8% against 95.4% at no per-call cost. In a live deployment judged by a user, hosting only the two generative sites matches hosting everything (39/43 against 39/43) for 28% of the spend, and the actuation gap the benchmark predicted appears as exactly one case in twenty-six.
