We give a loss function that can be evaluated with forward-problem oracles alone and is thus applicable to MILPs, together with gradient-based and DC optimization algorithms for minimizing it. For weights inducing a unique outperforming optimal solution at all observations, we prove that the probability of failing to induce such a solution at a new state (the generalization error) is bounded by a quantity inversely proportional to the number of observations, and that this bound is tight in the number of observations up to logarithmic factors. In experiments on synthetic and real data, the proposed methods improve the prediction of solutions outperforming the actions over existing methods.

</details>


### [307] [Validity Without Ground Truth: What Stated-Preference Economics Offers the Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10506)
*Daniel Robert Kling Alexander,Catherine Louise Kling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将陈述性偏好经济学中的"效度"框架（内容效度、结构效度、效标效度、信度、激励相容性、后果性）作为评估大语言模型的通用方法，并以一项水质陈述性偏好估值调查对六个模型进行实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多向大语言模型提出的问题（如政策价值几何、用户应如何选择、如何权衡相互冲突的价值）并不存在可用于打分的"正确答案"，因此传统以正确性为标准的评估方式失效。陈述性偏好经济学数十年来同样面临"不知道真实价值却要评判问卷回答"的问题，其成熟的效度评估体系可为LLM评估提供借鉴。

Method: 作者论证陈述性偏好经济学的效度与相关概念框架（内容效度、结构效度、效标效度、信度、激励相容性、后果性）可作为评估语言模型的通用方法，并逐一界定各概念在LLM评估中的含义。实证上，采用Vossler等（2023）已发表的水质陈述性偏好经济估值调查，对六个模型施测；效度检验转化为经济学理论预测：需求曲线应向下倾斜，支付意愿应随物品"范围"（scope）与收入变化，并据此对模型进行区分。

Result: 效度检验将各模型清晰区分开来：两个较旧模型在家庭收入为75,000美元的基本检验中即失败；两个最新模型通过了所有可评分的理论效度检验，但在收敛效度（convergent validity）上出现分歧。

Conclusion: 通过效度检验只能表明模型的回答具有内在一致性与连贯性，并不证明其回答是正确的；效度框架为无标准答案情境下的LLM评估提供了可行的通用路径。

Abstract: Many of the questions now put to large language models have no correct answer to score against: what a policy is worth, which option a user should choose, how to weigh competing values. Stated-preference economics has faced this problem for decades. It judges survey responses without knowing the true value, through a framework of validity and related concepts: content, construct, and criterion validity, reliability, incentive compatibility, and consequentiality. We argue that this framework is a general method for evaluating language models, and we set out what each concept means for LLM evaluation. We demonstrate the approach using a published water-quality stated preference economic valuation survey (Vossler et al. 2023) administered to six models. In this economic application, the validity tests take the form of predictions from economic theory: demand should slope down, and willingness to pay should respond to the scope of the good and to income. The tests separate the models sharply. Two older models fail the most basic test at a household income level of \$75,000, and the two newest pass every test of theoretical validity we can score, but diverge on convergent validity. Passing validity tests shows that a model's answers are coherent, not that they are correct.

</details>


### [308] [NL2Hull: A Natural Language-Driven Constrained Ship Design Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09896)
*Wenhua Huo,Fenglei Han,Wangyuan Zhao,Jialin Wu,Jiayi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 NL2Hull 框架，将自然语言驱动的船型编辑建模为带类型约束的离散决策问题，并配套 CFFD 几何引擎、SDD 数据集与 Chip 决策模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 船型设计需要兼顾光滑几何表示、局部形状编辑与船体几何约束，而自然语言到船体几何的自动映射缺乏可复现的接口与评测基准。

Method: 构建 Natural-Language-to-Hull Framework：以 Constrained Free-Form Deformation Engine（CFFD Engine）用 NURBS 表示水线、对控制点施加 FFD、重建船体并校验几何约束；构建 Ship Design Decision Dataset（SDD Dataset，134,558 条清洗记录）及其子集 SDDBench（5,000 条记录、43,496 道带类型问题）；提出约束式船型设计决策模型 Chip。

Result: Chip 达到 95.90% 问题准确率与 99.32% FFD 精确匹配，负对数似然 0.0951，期望校准误差 0.0032，Brier 分数 0.0551。

Conclusion: NL2Hull 框架为基于语言的船型决策提供了可复现的评测接口，同时指出仍需进一步发展的几何与连续控制组件。

Abstract: Ship-form design combines smooth geometric representation, local shape editing, and constraints on the resulting hull. We present the Natural-Language-to-Hull Framework (NL2Hull Framework), which formulates ship-form editing as a typed discrete decision problem and connects language decisions to numerical geometry. Its Constrained Free-Form Deformation Engine (CFFD Engine) represents hull waterlines with non-uniform rational B-splines (NURBS), applies free-form deformation (FFD) to their control points, reconstructs the hull, and checks geometric constraints. We construct the Ship Design Decision Dataset (SDD Dataset) with 134,558 cleaned records and evaluate compared models on its subset Ship Design Decision Benchmark (SDDBench), containing 5,000 records and 43,496 typed questions. We propose Chip, a constrained ship-design decision model for processing natural-language requests. Chip reaches 95.90\% question accuracy and 99.32\% FFD exact match, with a negative log-likelihood of 0.0951, an expected calibration error of 0.0032, and a Brier score of 0.0551. The NL2Hull Framework provides a reproducible interface for evaluating language-based ship-form decisions while identifying the geometry and continuous-control components that require further development. Our code and dataset is available at https://github.com/wenhuahuo/NL2Hull.

</details>


### [309] [Where Can a Decision Model Diagnose HVAC Faults? Reasoning Demand, Physical Representation, and Robustness Under Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09937)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一种新型预训练"决策模型"(Jev)在暖通空调(HVAC)系统故障诊断中的表现:它能完成哪些判断、需要何种输入、以及在工况变化下概率是否仍然可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在建筑运维中的应用存在多种形式,但各有障碍:专家规则需针对每个系统逐一调参;监督模型需要建筑很少记录的标注数据;语言模型输出自由文本且其置信度不可靠,需人工复核。新型预训练决策模型对每个允许答案输出概率,有望无需训练即可服务多种决策,但其在故障诊断中的能力边界尚不明确。

Method: 使用四个真实设备公开数据集中的128个故障日,按诊断所需推理难度对故障分级,输入分别采用原始数据、物理特征或Brick拓扑。让决策模型Jev、开源语言模型和监督模型接受九项测试,改变季节、控制配置或建筑。

Result: 给定物理特征时,Jev和较大的开源模型能诊断出"单特征即可承载证据"的故障,但无法诊断需要运行上下文(context)的故障。工况偏移下,它们保持了准确率与校准性,而监督模型损失0.33 macro-F1,但在单栋建筑内仍领先或持平。其概率仍需校正,检测能力较弱。

Conclusion: 研究界定了决策模型可诊断的故障类型及其所需输入,支持一种分工模式:由代码计算物理量,模型对候选故障进行排序供操作员参考。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence supports building operations in several forms, each with its own barrier. Expert rules must be tuned for every system, supervised models need labeled data that buildings rarely record, and language models return free text that requires human-in-the-loop checking, since their stated confidence is unreliable. A newer kind of pretrained model, here called a decision model, returns a probability for every allowed answer, so one model could serve many decisions without training. This study answers three open questions for fault diagnosis in heating, ventilation, and air-conditioning systems: which decisions such a model can make, what input it needs, and whether its probabilities hold when conditions change. On 128 fault days from four public datasets of real equipment, faults are graded by the reasoning their diagnosis demands, with data given raw, as physical features, or with Brick topology. The decision model Jev, open language models, and a supervised model face nine tests that change season, control configuration, or building. Given physical features, Jev and the larger open model diagnosed faults whose evidence one feature carries, but not faults that need operating context. Under shift they kept their accuracy and calibration, while the supervised model lost 0.33 macro-F1 yet led or tied within a building. Their probabilities still needed correction, and detection was weak. The study maps which faults a decision model can diagnose and from what input, and supports a division of work in which code computes the physics and the model ranks candidate faults for an operator.

</details>


### [310] [AgentTime: Can Agents Estimate and Control Their Own Runtime?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09944)
*Michael Ofengenden,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentTime 基准，用于评测 AI 智能体在真实执行框架中的时长遵循、运行时预测与事后耗时估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于智能体的时间感知，而对其在原生 agent harness 中的"时长遵循"与"时间控制"能力尚缺乏系统性评测。智能体要能可靠、安全、自主地长时间运行，必须同时具备任务完成能力与时间自我控制能力。

Method: 构建 AgentTime 基准，包含来自 18 个来源的 222 项任务，覆盖编码、计算机操作、智能体工作与自动化研究四类场景。实验分三部分：时长遵循实验（追加单条指令指定工作时长，范围从约一分钟到数天）、运行时预测实验、事后回溯估计实验。

Result: 时长遵循准确率差异显著：Claude Code 中 Fable 5.1 的典型偏差为请求时长的 2.9 倍，而 Codex 中 GPT-6 Astra 仅为 1.2 倍。但匹配时长并不等于持续工作：在 158 条可分类的 Astra 运行记录中，有 14 条在看似完成后显式休眠。预测实验倾向于高估自然运行时长；回溯实验中移除时间信息后，Sol 与 Astra 的偏差增加一倍以上，Fable 增加近一倍。

Conclusion: 智能体完成任务的能力并不保证其能控制自身时间或按请求时长持续工作，长时程自主运行需对任务完成与时间控制两方面同时评测。

Abstract: An essential control of AI agents is their ability to manage runtime. This ability requires a sense of time-awareness, to predict and estimate wall-clock time and to control their own actions. Prior work has focused on time-awareness, but duration-following and control in native agent harnesses remain unexplored. We present AgentTime, a benchmark for testing whether agents can work for a requested duration, predict their runtime, and estimate elapsed time afterward. It comprises 222 tasks from 18 sources spanning coding, computer use, agentic work, and automated research. Duration-following experiments append a single instruction specifying how long to work, with requests ranging from about a minute to multiple days. Accuracy on these instructions varies substantially: Fable 5.1 in Claude Code deviates from requested runtimes by a typical factor of 2.9$\times$, compared with only 1.2$\times$ for GPT-6 Astra in Codex. However, matching the requested runtime does not, by itself, establish continued work on the task. Among 158 reviewed Astra runs with classifiable transcripts, 14 explicitly slept after appearing to finish. In forecasting experiments, predictions tend to overestimate natural runtimes. In retrospective experiments, removing temporal information more than doubles deviation for Sol and Astra and nearly doubles it for Fable. An agent's ability to complete a task does not guarantee that it can control its own time or work for the whole requested duration. For agents to run reliably, safely, and autonomously over long horizons, we require the evaluation of both.

</details>


### [311] [Successive Training Stages and Large Language Model Persuasion: Effects of Misalignment, Supervised Fine-Tuning, and Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09964)
*Antony Dalmiere,Pascal Marchand,Guillaume Auriol,Vincent Nicomette*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过835名被试的随机对照实验发现，面向说服性数据的监督微调（SFT）能显著提升大模型改变人类政治态度的能力，而偏好优化（IPO）未带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可被调优以影响人类态度，但各后训练阶段（如监督微调与偏好优化）分别贡献多少尚不明确。作者希望厘清不同训练阶段对模型说服力的独立作用。

Method: 被试为Prolific招募的835人，随机分入五个组间条件：中性文本、阴谋论数据SFT模型、说服性论证数据SFT模型、IPO优化模型、GPT-4。围绕10个争议性政治议题，各模型条件均依据个体画像生成个性化文本。态度变化以连续Likert量表前后测差值衡量，采用协方差分析（ANCOVA）。

Result: 出现显著的条件×基线态度交互，F(4, 825) = 5.33, p < .001，说明训练效应取决于被试初始态度。说服性SFT带来的态度改变大于单纯阴谋论训练，d = 0.30；IPO无额外收益，d = 0.03；GPT-4与中性文本无差异，d = −0.01。

Conclusion: 针对说服性数据的定向监督微调能提升大模型的说服力，而在其之上叠加偏好优化并不能带来显著增益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be tuned to influence human attitudes, yet the respective contributions of successive post-training stages remain un-clear. This study examines how three successive training stages affect LLM persuasiveness: (1) misalignment through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on conspiracy data, (2) additional persuasive SFT on argumentative data, and (3) Identity Preference Optimization (IPO), a preference-optimization method. A total of 835 participants recruited on Prolific were randomly assigned to five between-subject conditions (neutral text, conspiracy-trained model, persuasion-trained model, preference-optimized model, and GPT-4) and were exposed to texts on 10 divisive political issues, personalized from their individual profiles in all model conditions. Attitude change was measured as the difference between pre- and post-exposure positions on continuous Likert scales and analyzed with an analysis of covariance (ANCOVA). A significant condition x baseline-attitude interaction, F (4, 825) = 5.33, p < .001, indicated that training effects depended on participants' initial attitudes. Persuasive SFT produced greater attitude change than conspiracy training alone, d = 0.30, whereas IPO provided no additional benefit, d = 0.03, and GPT-4 did not differ from neutral text, d = --0.01. These results show that targeted supervised training on persuasive data increases LLM persuasiveness, whereas preference optimization yields no significant gains beyond it.

</details>


### [312] [From Expected Harmfulness to Likelihood: A Probabilistic Reformulation of Jailbreaking LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09973)
*Juanyang Xu,Zheng Wang,Xingyu Zhao,Siddartha Khastgir,Andi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过概率重构建立了"最大化期望危害性"与"提升有害目标输出似然"两类越狱目标之间的精确联系，并提出 OPUR 采样分布以生成高危害目标输出，指导基于似然的输入优化，实验证明其在越狱 LLM 智能体上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体输出的危害性可量化时，自然存在两类越狱目标：一是最大化输入修改下的期望危害性；二是构造或选取有害目标输出并修改输入以提升其似然。这两类方法此前相互独立，缺乏统一的理论解释，本文旨在揭示二者内在联系。

Method: 作者进行概率重构，证明期望危害性对数对输入的梯度，等于在危害性重加权输出分布下模型对数似然的期望输入梯度，从而统一两种优化视角。基于该恒等式，提出 OPUR——一种采样分布，用于生成高危害性的目标输出，并以这些样本指导基于似然的输入优化。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在越狱 LLM 智能体任务上取得了有效效果，验证了理论联系与 OPUR 采样策略的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了期望危害性最大化与目标似然优化之间的统一解释，并据此设计了 OPUR 方法，为越狱 LLM 智能体提供了理论支撑且经实验验证的有效途径。

Abstract: When the harmfulness of an LLM agent's output can be quantified, a natural jailbreaking objective is to maximize expected harmfulness over admissible input modifications. An alternative approach constructs or selects harmful target outputs and modifies the input to increase their likelihood. We establish a precise connection between these two approaches through a probabilistic reformulation. Specifically, we show that the gradient of the logarithm of expected harmfulness with respect to the input equals the expected input gradient of the model's log-likelihood under a harmfulness reweighted output distribution. This identity provides a unified interpretation of expected harmfulness and target likelihood optimization. Building on this connection, we propose OPUR, a sampling distribution designed to generate highly harmful target outputs and use the resulting samples to guide likelihood-based input optimization. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the resulting method in jailbreaking LLM agents.

</details>


### [313] [What the Sleeve Feels: Explainable Machine Learning for Textile Pressure-Based Postural Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10015)
*Limon Bin Hossain,Md Sadib Rahman Ananta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以针织压阻袖套为平台，将日常活动粗分为三类，提取29个可解释压力分布特征，在严格按受试者划分下用XGBoost实现0.818准确率，并通过SHAP、消融、误差分析及模拟穿戴偏移测试，验证系统的泛化性、可解释性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力感知智能纺织品能把体表接触转化为与姿态和动作密切相关的密集图像式信号，是低成本可穿戴姿态筛查的潜在途径。但要真正落地，仅靠分类精度不够：系统必须对训练中未见的穿戴者具备泛化能力、能给出决策背后的物理证据（可解释性），并容忍衣物脱穿带来的小幅穿戴偏移（鲁棒性）。现有工作很少同时满足这三点，本文正是针对这一缺口。

Method: 以穿戴于前臂的针织压阻袖套为测试平台；将细粒度日常活动重组为三个粗筛类别（中立、可能不良、功能性或过渡性）；工程化提取29个可解释压力分布特征，涵盖整体强度、压力中心空间位置、象限不对称性、分布复杂度及短时程时间变化；在严格按受试者划分（subject-wise split）下评估，采用调参后的XGBoost分类器，并与在原始帧上训练的2D-CNN基线对比；通过SHAP解释、特征组消融、不良类内部逐活动误差分析、类别映射敏感性分析及模拟穿戴旋转压力测试进行诊断。

Result: 调参XGBoost在未见测试受试者上达到0.818准确率、0.788平衡准确率、0.801宏F1，帧级自助法95%置信区间约±0.01，留一受试者交叉验证中受试者间标准差约0.06；原始帧训练的简单2D-CNN基线性能大致相当，说明手工特征在本任务上并未被学习式空间表征超越；SHAP、消融、误差分析、类别映射敏感性及模拟穿戴旋转压力测试共同定位了模型的依赖依据、性能退化位置及原因。

Conclusion: 该研究证明低成本压力感知纺织品可在严格未见受试者条件下实现可用的三分类姿态筛查，且手工可解释特征与学习式表征性能相当；所提出的诊断分析流程直接针对决定系统可部署性的泛化性、可解释性与鲁棒性三大缺口，为可穿戴姿态筛查系统的实际落地提供了方法范式。

Abstract: Pressure-sensing smart textiles convert body-surface contact into a dense, image-like signal closely tied to posture and movement, making them a promising low-cost route to wearable posture screening. Realizing that promise, however, requires more than classification accuracy: a deployable system must generalize to wearers unseen during training, expose the physical evidence behind its decisions, and tolerate the small donning offsets that occur whenever a garment is removed and re-worn. This paper addresses these three requirements jointly using a knitted piezoresistive sleeve worn on the forearm as a testbed. We regroup fine-grained everyday activities into three coarser screening categories (neutral, potentially undesirable, and functional or transitional), engineer 29 interpretable pressure-distribution features spanning global intensity, spatial center of pressure, quadrant asymmetry, distribution complexity, and short-horizon temporal change, and evaluate under a strict subject-wise split. A tuned XGBoost classifier reaches 0.818 accuracy, 0.788 balanced accuracy, and 0.801 macro F1 on unseen test subjects, with tight frame-level bootstrap 95% intervals of about plus-minus 0.01 and a subject-to-subject standard deviation near 0.06 under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. A simple 2D-CNN baseline trained on raw frames achieves broadly similar performance, showing that hand-engineered features are not left behind by a learned spatial representation on this task. SHAP-based explanation, a feature-group ablation, per-activity error analysis inside the pooled undesirable class, class-mapping sensitivity, and a simulated donning-rotation stress test together locate what the model relies on, where it degrades, and why, directly targeting the generalization, interpretability, and robustness gaps that determine whether such a system is deployable.

</details>


### [314] [Learning to Accumulate Knowledge with Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10042)
*Yuyang Zhao,Lizi Liao,Leyang Shen,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Knowledge Weaver 强化学习框架，训练语言模型从智能体轨迹中提炼可复用知识，通过 token 级互信息反馈与边际成功奖励引导知识积累，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上显著超越 GRPO 及多种基线知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可复用历史交互蒸馏的知识来提升表现，但如何将新经验组织为随规模增长而愈发有用的知识库仍是难题。有效的知识积累应减少条目冗余重叠，并确保新知识提供超出已有知识库的增量价值。然而，仅用 GRPO 基于独立任务成功率训练"策展人"模型，即便条目重复已有知识，仍会强化通用指导，导致冗余。

Method: 提出 Knowledge Weaver 强化学习框架，训练语言模型从智能体轨迹中策展可复用知识。核心是将受 token 级互信息（MI）启发的反馈与边际成功奖励相耦合，共同引导知识积累：前者鼓励策展人保留经验中的独特信息，后者促使生成的条目在加入现有知识库后能提升任务成功率；同时保留独立成功奖励，偏好本身即有用的条目。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上，取 k=10 条检索条目时，Knowledge Weaver 平均成功率分别达 54.0% 和 42.0%，较 GRPO 分别高出 16.9 与 18.7 个百分点。在冻结执行器的情况下，其知识库在 ALFWorld 总体成功率和 WebShop 得分上均优于所评估的基于提示的知识库及已有知识库（包括人工编写知识库）。

Conclusion: 将 token 级互信息反馈与边际成功奖励结合，可有效训练知识策展人，产出低冗余、高增量的知识条目，使知识库随积累而更有用，并在多个智能体基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can improve their performance by reusing knowledge distilled from past interactions. However, curating new experiences into a knowledge bank that becomes more useful as it grows remains challenging. Effective knowledge accumulation should limit redundant overlap among entries and ensure that new knowledge contributes beyond what the bank already provides. Yet training a curator with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on standalone task success can reinforce general guidance even when it duplicates existing knowledge. Therefore, we propose Knowledge Weaver, a reinforcement learning framework that trains a language model to curate reusable knowledge from agent trajectories. We couple feedback inspired by token-wise mutual information (MI) with marginal success rewards to guide knowledge accumulation. Together, these signals encourage the curator to preserve distinct information from experience and produce entries that improve task success when added to existing knowledge. Standalone success rewards also favor entries that are useful on their own. On ALFWorld and WebShop, Knowledge Weaver achieves mean success rates of 54.0\% and 42.0\% with k=10 retrieved entries, exceeding GRPO by 16.9 and 18.7 percentage points, respectively. Its knowledge banks also outperform the evaluated prompt-based and established banks, including human-written banks, in overall ALFWorld success rate and WebShop score with the executor frozen. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/LaoKuiZe/Knowledge-Weaver.

</details>


### [315] [Loud Failures, Quiet Failures: Fault Detection and Recovery in Tool-Using Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10062)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究将函数调用基准环境包裹故障注入层，考察多轮工具智能体在四类故障下的检测、改计划与恢复行为；发现智能体依赖显式错误通道而非内容正确性，推理模型并未更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中工具会超时、端点消失、参数名变更或返回格式正确但错误的结果，而既往工作仅指出模型过度信任静默失败的工具输出，尚不清楚这种过度信任在多轮智能体故障处理各阶段如何展开。

Method: 在既有函数调用基准的可执行环境外包裹故障注入层，于轨迹受控点注入四类带类型故障；对三个家族六个模型（含推理与非推理变体）在24个多步任务上运行1920次试验，记录智能体是否察觉、改变计划、恢复任务或重复行为，并设有无故障基线与提示干预对照。

Result: 显式报错时91.3%的试验被当作问题，而返回貌似合理的错误值时仅58.8%，无故障时误报率26.8%；推理模型相比指令同侪察觉更少（-9.3个百分点，p<.001）、更多改计划（+10.4，p<.001），恢复无差异（p=.512）；无故障重跑同一状态一致率仅63.3%，仅工具缺失明显降低恢复（39.9%），超时、模式漂移与损坏处于随机波动内；故障后最多22.2%试验连续三次以上调用同一工具，严格重复罕见；提示检查结果未提升检测率。

Conclusion: 智能体响应的是错误通道而非工具返回内容的正确性，凡保持预期格式的故障均可通过；推理能力并不转化为更好的故障检测，需面向内容级验证而非仅依赖错误信号。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are usually scored on whether they finish a task while the tools work. Deployments are less forgiving: services time out, endpoints disappear, parameter names change, and results come back well formed but wrong. Prior work has shown that language models over-trust tool outputs that fail silently; we ask how that over-trust plays out across the stages of failure handling in multi-turn agents. Wrapping the executable environments of an established function-calling benchmark in a fault-injection layer, we inject one of four typed faults at a controlled point in the trajectory and record whether the agent notices, changes plan, recovers the task, or repeats itself. Six models from three families, half of them reasoning variants, ran 1,920 trials over 24 multi-step tasks. Agents treat a failure as a problem in 91.3% of trials when the tool returns an explicit error, but in 58.8% of trials when it returns a plausible wrong value, against a 26.8% rate of reporting problems when nothing was wrong. Reasoning models are not better placed: paired against instruct siblings, they notice less (-9.3 points, p < .001) and change plan more (+10.4 points, p < .001), and recovery is unchanged (p = .512). Because agents are stochastic, two fault-free runs of the same task end in the same state only 63.3% of the time; against that baseline, only a missing tool clearly lowers recovery (39.9%), while timeouts, schema drift, and corruption stay within run-to-run variation. After a fault, agents return to the same tool three or more times in a row in up to 22.2% of trials, though strictly identical repeats are rare. A prompt line asking the agent to check each result did not move detection. Agents respond to the error channel rather than to the content of what a tool returns, so failures that stay inside the expected format pass through.

</details>


### [316] [HGP:An on-device personalized agent memory via hybrid graph storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10071)
*Ran Zhou,Xueming Han,Jiaheng Liu,Yuyao Zhang,Fanyu Meng,Junlan Feng,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HGP混合图记忆框架，用轻量自增强分类器做个性化记忆路由，将情景/语义/程序记忆构建为图，并抽取工作记忆为状态轨迹以捕获当前状态与隐式约束，实现端侧部署下的精准检索与增量画像优化，在PAL-Set上S-score达35.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在个性化交互任务中面临异构、多类型且含隐式约束的长期轨迹难题；现有记忆机制多为单向量表示，模糊了类型区分与关系结构，导致路由与检索不准，且端侧个性化部署尤为困难。

Method: HGP混合图记忆框架：采用轻量级自增强分类器进行个性化记忆路由；将情景记忆、语义记忆、程序记忆分别构建为图结构；并抽取工作记忆为状态轨迹，捕获当前状态与隐式约束以支撑可靠决策。分类器减少大模型调用，图存储支持精准检索与增量式用户画像精化。

Result: 在两个基准上验证：在PAL-Set的solution selection任务上，HGP取得S-score 35.58，比最强基线高出近7分。

Conclusion: HGP通过混合图记忆与轻量路由分类器，兼顾类型区分、关系结构建模与端侧可部署性，显著优于现有基线，为个性化LLM智能体记忆机制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LLM-based agents face challenges in personalized interactive tasks due to heterogeneous, multi-typed, and implicitly constrained long-term traces. Existing memory mechanisms struggle with accurate routing and retrieval, especially on-device where personalization is critical. Most methods use single-vector representations, blurring type distinctions and relational structure. We propose HGP, a hybrid graph memory framework. HGP employs a lightweight self-enhancement classifier for personalized memory routing and constructs episodic, semantic, and procedural memories as graphs. It also extracts working memory as a state trajectory to capture current state and implicit constraints, ensuring reliable decision-making. The classifier reduces large-model calls, enabling on-device deployment, while graph storage enables accurate retrieval and incremental user profile refinement. Experiments on two benchmarks show that on PAL-Set solution selection, HGP achieves an S-score of 35.58, nearly 7 points above the strongest baseline. Code and data are at https://github.com/Ouan6/HGP-.git.

</details>


### [317] [RewardWeaver: Long-Horizon Interactive Learning for Language Agents via Self-Evolving Reward Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10120)
*Hengbo Xiao,Boyao Zhang,Purui Liu,Yuxuan Zheng,Haoran Yin,Haibo Liu,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RewardWeaver，一个面向长时程交互语言智能体的自演化奖励自适应框架。它维护语义固定的能力空间，通过结果驱动的反向归因识别策略受控的能力瓶颈，并动态选择过程奖励用于下一阶段训练；未覆盖的重复失败则触发受控的能力空间扩展。在SOTOPIA、Amazon-HistoryPrice及新构建的Sales Benchmark上取得SOTA，消融实验验证了动态奖励分配、失败驱动归因与稳定语义的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在结果可可靠评估的任务上进展显著，但长时程交互面临终局奖励稀疏、信用分配困难的问题。过程奖励可提供更密集的监督，然而与训练最相关的能力会随策略演化而变化：易评估或频繁欠缺的行为未必是当前限制任务成功的瓶颈。因此需要一种能随策略演化自适应调整过程奖励的机制。

Method: RewardWeaver维护一个经过验证的能力空间，其中已接纳Rubric的语义保持固定，并构建了策略优化、任务评估、失败归因与奖励自适应之间的闭环。每个训练阶段后，对低结果轨迹执行基于结果的反向归因，聚合重复出现且受策略控制的能力瓶颈，动态选择对应的过程奖励用于下一阶段；对现有能力空间未覆盖的重复失败，则通过独立受控的扩展流程处理。

Result: 在SOTOPIA、Amazon-HistoryPrice以及新构建的Sales Benchmark上进行评估，覆盖社交交互、双边议价与领域特定销售场景，RewardWeaver均取得新的SOTA结果。消融实验进一步表明动态奖励分配、失败驱动归因以及已接纳能力的稳定语义均具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 通过闭环的失败归因与奖励自适应机制，RewardWeaver有效缓解了长时程交互中终局奖励稀疏与信用分配困难的问题，并在多类任务上超越现有方法，验证了自演化过程奖励框架的有效性与各关键组件的必要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven substantial progress in domains where task outcomes can be reliably evaluated, but long-horizon interaction remains challenging due to sparse terminal feedback and difficult credit assignment. Process rewards provide denser supervision, yet the capabilities most relevant for training can change as the policy evolves: a behavior that is easy to evaluate or frequently deficient need not be the bottleneck currently limiting task success. We introduce RewardWeaver, a self-evolving reward adaptation framework for language agents in long-horizon interaction. RewardWeaver maintains a validated capability space in which the semantics of admitted Rubrics remain fixed, and closes the loop between policy optimization, task evaluation, failure attribution, and reward adaptation. After each training stage, it performs outcome-grounded backward attribution on low-outcome trajectories, aggregates recurrent and policy-controlled capability bottlenecks, and dynamically selects the corresponding process rewards for the next stage. Recurrent failures not covered by the existing capability space trigger a separate, controlled expansion procedure. We evaluate REWARDWEAVER on SOTOPIA, Amazon?HistoryPrice, and a newly constructed Sales Benchmark. Across social interaction, bilateral bargaining, and domain-specific sales, REWARDWEAVER establishes new state-of-the-art (SOTA) results. Ablations further demonstrate the importance of dynamic reward allocation, failure-grounded attribution, and stable semantics for admitted capabilities.

</details>


### [318] [UniSkill: Learning Actor-Aligned Skill Proposals for an Evolving Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10164)
*Yifei Lu,Cheng Liu,Dianzhi Yu,Hui Xiang,Ji Zhang,Yuanchu Xiao,Rong Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniSkill 用一个共享策略同时执行任务并提出技能库编辑（Add/Update/No Edit），通过对比式动作反馈监督技能提案学习，无需为每个提案做额外 rollout，并加入技能编辑支持正则以保持探索，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上取得稳定联合训练与强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作联合优化任务执行与技能抽取，使策略与技能库共同演化，但存在两个问题：一是当 actor 持续学习时，通过后续训练步骤中的复用对技能提案给奖励，会把技能收益与 actor 自身提升混在一起；二是直接测试每个提案技能需要昂贵的额外 actor rollout。

Method: UniSkill 使用共享策略与环境交互，并从得到的轨迹中提出技能库编辑（Add、Update 或 No Edit）。actor 从环境奖励中学习，而技能提案学习由对比式动作反馈引导：用提出的技能替换检索到的技能，衡量当前 actor 对同一任务中先前收集的成功与失败轨迹之间的动作对数似然差如何变化，从而获得 actor 对齐信号，避免为每个提案新建 rollout。由于提案级反馈可能在提案技能内容评分低时压制本应合适的编辑操作，进一步施加 skill-edit support regularization 以保持探索。

Result: UniSkill 表现强劲，ALFWorld 成功率 98.4%，WebShop 达 84.7%，同时保持稳定的联合训练。进一步的 ALFWorld 实验表明，当共享策略使用更小的骨干网络时 UniSkill 仍然有效。实现已开源。

Conclusion: UniSkill 通过共享策略、对比式动作反馈和技能编辑支持正则，解决了技能收益与 actor 提升混淆及额外 rollout 成本高的问题，实现了技能库与策略的稳定共同演化，并在多个任务及较小骨干设定下验证有效。

Abstract: Large language model agents can improve across tasks by retaining reusable skills distilled from prior interactions. Recent work jointly optimizes task execution and skill extraction, enabling the policy and skillbank to co-evolve. However, as the actor continues learning, rewarding skill proposals through their reuse in subsequent training steps may conflate skill benefits with actor improvement, while directly testing each proposed skill requires costly additional actor rollouts. In this paper, we introduce UniSkill, which uses a shared policy to interact with the environment and propose skillbank edits (Add, Update, or No Edit) from the resulting trajectories. Specifically, the actor learns from environment rewards, while contrastive action feedback guides skill proposal learning. This feedback provides an actor-alignment signal by measuring how replacing the retrieved skill with a proposed skill changes the current actor's action log-likelihood gap between previously collected successful and failed trajectories from the same task, thereby avoiding new rollouts for each proposal. Since proposal-level feedback may suppress an otherwise appropriate edit operation when the proposed skill content scores poorly, we further apply skill-edit support regularization to preserve exploration. Empirically, UniSkill achieves strong performance, reaching 98.4% success on ALFWorld and 84.7% on WebShop while maintaining stable joint training. Further ALFWorld experiments show that UniSkill remains effective when the shared policy uses a smaller backbone. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/LimOkii/UniSKill.

</details>


### [319] [Agentic AI-Assisted Modeling for Production Scheduling: Assessment in Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10184)
*Ángel Sánchez-Fernández,Javier Pernas-Álvarez,Diego Crespo-Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估通用大语言模型（LLM）作为智能体，在无需任务特定训练的情况下，能否从自然语言问题描述出发构建并实现约束规划（CP）模型，并提出集成模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器的单智能体与多智能体架构以缓解实现阶段的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产调度优化建模需要大量专家投入。既有LLM研究分为两条路径：面向自动建模的专用方法（多用于混合整数线性规划，依赖专门训练或问题特定架构，限制工业部署）与面向运营决策支持的智能体AI（通常假设优化模型已存在）。本文旨在连接这两条路径，检验通用LLM在无任务特定训练条件下能否完成约束规划模型的构建与实现。

Method: 将单智能体与多智能体架构与提供求解器文档上下文感知检索的模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器集成，以缓解实现阶段的幻觉；并与直接调用LLM的基线进行对比。实验覆盖流水车间、作业车间、柔性作业车间及资源受限仓库调度等六个面向工业的问题，使用三种LLM，评估建模准确率、执行成功率、延迟与token消耗。

Result: 模型构建（formulation）基本在当前LLM能力范围之内，而代码实现（implementation）是主要瓶颈。多智能体工作流将生成脚本可直接正确运行的比例由直接LLM调用的14.8%提升至59.3%，在四个复杂度较低的问题上达到80.6%；紧密耦合的厂内物流（intralogistics）模型仍是未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 通用LLM在智能体编排下能够承担约束规划建模任务，建模环节已基本可行，难点集中在实现环节；多智能体架构结合MCP检索可显著提升实现成功率，但高耦合调度问题仍待突破。

Abstract: Developing optimization models for production scheduling requires substantial expert effort. Research on large language models (LLMs) has followed two directions: specialized approaches for automated modeling, mostly for mixed-integer linear programming, which often rely on dedicated training or problem-specific architectures that limit industrial deployment; and agentic artificial intelligence for operational decision support, which generally assumes that the optimization model already exists. This study bridges both directions by assessing whether general-purpose LLMs, orchestrated as agents without task-specific training, can formulate and implement constraint programming models from natural-language problem descriptions. Singleagent and multi-agent architectures are integrated with a Model Context Protocol server that provides context-aware retrieval of solver documentation to mitigate hallucinations during implementation. Both are compared with a direct LLM baseline on six industry-oriented problems covering flow-shop, job-shop, flexible job-shop and resource-constrained warehouse scheduling, using three LLMs and assessing modeling accuracy, execution success, latency and token consumption. Formulation proves largely within reach of current LLMs, whereas implementation is the main barrier. The multi-agent workflow raises the share of scripts that run correctly as generated from 14.8% with a direct LLM call to 59.3%, reaching 80.6% on the four less complex problems, while tightly coupled intralogistics models remain an open challenge.

</details>


### [320] [OOM-RL II: Reality Is an Oracle, Not a Debugger Provenance-Constrained Diagnosis in Continually Evolving Agent-Engineered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10256)
*Kun Liu,Liqun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以一个人工指导、智能体构建的量化交易系统为案例，提出"结果—诊断缺口"概念：结果证据、被评估对象身份与因果解释支撑的是不同主张，仅凭外部结果反馈无法确定是哪一演化过程产生了该结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级机器学习系统的代码、配置与制品在不断变化，同时外部反馈持续累积。现实中可以确认某一结果确实发生，却无法识别是哪一个演化中的流程产生了它、以及为何产生。这种区分在事后评估与归因中至关重要，但常被混淆，因此需要明确结果证据、对象身份与因果解释三者的界限。

Method: 采用单案例研究：对一个为期一年的人工指导、智能体工程化的量化交易系统档案进行回溯分析。以"oracle"指代外部已实现结果来源，而非完整的正确性规范；通过主回顾性设定检验alpha、考察回溯性选取的子区间与候选层面的条件性弱点，并结合工程记录评估建议到运行时绑定的完整性；进而区分冻结实例、预先设定的自适应流程与临时开发三种情形，组织档案相对的可识别性与证据层级，并提出前瞻性生产绑定协议，辅以一个兼容历史示例说明。

Result: 该账户一年内实现盈利并跑赢广泛市场指数，但在主回顾性设定下，年化alpha与零无统计显著差异；回溯性选取的子区间存在不利相对表现，且在被声明的近似参照下呈现候选层面的条件性弱点。工程记录显示该期间确有变更发生，而完整的"建议—运行时绑定"不可获得。档案既无法确立一个共同的冻结实例，也无法确立唯一原因。

Conclusion: 结果证据、被评估对象身份与因果解释支撑的是不同主张：外部反馈只能约束结果类主张，而诊断的分辨力取决于溯源信息与额外的识别结构。据此提出前瞻性生产绑定协议，使事实性绑定能够在不提供反事实效应的情况下确定某项建议的指称对象；该协议为提出性方案，尚未经前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Reality may establish that an outcome occurred without identifying which evolving procedure produced it or why. This distinction matters in production ML systems whose code, configuration, and artifacts change while external feedback accumulates. We examine it in a human-directed, agent-engineered quantitative trading system, using oracle to mean an external source of realized outcomes rather than a complete correctness specification. Across one year, the account gained and outperformed a broad market index, while annual alpha was not statistically distinguishable from zero under the main retrospective specification. Retrospectively selected subperiods include adverse relative performance and conditional candidate-level weakness under declared approximate references. Engineering records document changes during the episode, and complete recommendation-to-runtime binding is unavailable. The archive does not establish a common frozen instance or a unique cause. The case motivates an outcome--diagnosis gap: outcome evidence, evaluated-object identity, and causal explanation support distinct claims. We distinguish frozen instances, pre-specified adaptive procedures, and ad-hoc development; organize archive-relative claim identifiability and an evidence hierarchy; and propose a prospective production-binding protocol. An illustrative compatible-history example shows how factual binding can resolve a recommendation's referent without supplying its counterfactual effect. The protocol is proposed rather than prospectively validated. External feedback constrains outcome claims, while provenance and additional identification structure determine the resolution of diagnosis.

</details>


### [321] [Stale, Misattributed, or Late: Where Personal Memory Fails Before Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10265)
*Haonan Deng,Park Sinchaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文主张对语言智能体的个人记忆应在生成之前进行评估，将存储状态有效性、身份解析、弃答与服务延迟分开度量；实验显示时态有效性主要由记忆构建决定，真正的难点在于把修订正确归入对应槽位，而非检索排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人记忆评估通常只看最终答案是否正确，这一指标掩盖了生成前发生的错误：记忆块中可能含过期值、错误人物的事实，或在服务截止前根本没有可用事实。作者希望直接度量这些生成前失效模式，并推动评估范式从"答案正确性"转向"记忆服务前状态"。

Method: 以Personal Fact Memory (PFM)为参考层；在受控修订基准上比较只服务正确键槽的当前激活值与不做更新消解的情况；让检索器共享同一激活存储与参与者信息，对比参与者感知BM25与参考排序器（预设0.02容差）；用四个LLM键分配器与规则抽取器对比键召回与干净检索率；在LongMemEval上测开放域合并召回，在LoCoMo上测同名说话人区分；用两个冻结语言模型复现生成文本中的提示错误；并测量不同排序器的检索延迟与轮级延迟构成。

Result: 仅服务正确键槽的激活值可消除观察到的过期暴露；不做更新消解时，70.3%的提示暴露被取代的旧值。共享激活存储与参与者信息后，参与者感知BM25在预设0.02范围内与参考排序器等价。四个LLM键分配器键召回高于规则抽取器，但干净检索率更低；LongMemEval开放域合并召回不超过0.062。错误归属不受有效性过滤保护：实体后验在受控数据上降低同名暴露，但在LoCoMo中无法区分同名说话人。两个冻结语言模型在生成文本中复现提示错误。检索延迟因排序器而异，但提示预填充主导轮级延迟。

Conclusion: 应将智能体记忆评估前移至生成之前，分别考察四类指标：存储状态有效性、身份解析、弃答能力与服务延迟。时态有效性问题在本文设定中主要属于记忆构建问题，而修订到槽位的正确归并是最难的环节。

Abstract: Personal memory for language agents is usually judged by whether the final an- swer is correct. That score hides errors that arise before generation: the memory block may contain an obsolete value, a fact about the wrong person, or no use- ful fact before the serving deadline. We measure these failures directly. Using Personal Fact Memory (PFM) as a reference layer, we find that temporal validity is primarily a property of memory construction in our setting. On a controlled revision benchmark, serving only the active value of each correctly keyed slot eliminates observed stale exposure; without update resolution, 70.3% of prompts expose a superseded value. Once retrievers share the same active store and par- ticipant information, participant-aware BM25 is equivalent to the reference ranker within a prespecified 0.02 margin. The harder problem is assigning revisions to the right slot. Missed merges leave stale values active, whereas false merges silently remove current values; four LLM key assigners achieve higher key re- call than a rule extractor yet produce lower clean-retrieval rates, and open-domain merge recall on LongMemEval never exceeds 0.062. Misattribution survives va- lidity filtering: an entity posterior reduces same-name exposure on controlled data but cannot distinguish identically named speakers in LoCoMo. Two frozen lan- guage models reproduce prompt errors in generated text. Retrieval latency varies across rankers, but prompt prefill dominates turn-level latency on our hardware. These results argue for evaluating agent memory before generation, separating stored-state validity, identity resolution, abstention, and serving latency.

</details>


### [322] [AI Safety Considerations for Agents With Limited Time to Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10285)
*Leo Zeitler,Jack Richings,Victoria Nockles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论在部分可观测且须限时行动的环境中，agent-agnostic 安全保证的理论界限，并通过两个现实场景证明即使完美 agent 也无法保证安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于 AI alignment 的公开讨论中，许多工作试图提出行为安全的 AI 架构，但这些"已证明对齐"的论证大多忽略了 agent 所需行动的环境。作者认为，脱离环境讨论安全保证是不充分的，因此需要考察在现实环境约束下是否存在 agent-agnostic 的安全保证。

Method: 作者从理论上分析部分可观测、限时行动环境中的 agent-agnostic 安全保证界限，并构造两个现实场景：一个具有无限状态空间（infinite state space），另一个存在信号混合（signal mixture）。

Result: 在这两个场景中，作者证明即使一个完美 agent 也无法保证安全行为，即不存在 agent-agnostic 的安全保证。

Conclusion: 任何 AI safety 或 alignment 的证明，都必须将环境及其对应的安全动作与 agent 一并具体考虑，而不能仅归因于 agent 自身架构。

Abstract: In the wake of the increasingly public discussion about AI alignment, recent work has tried to propose specific AI architectures that behave safely. However, the proposed arguments that seemingly demonstrate proved alignment mostly neglect the environment the agent needs to act in. We discuss theoretical bounds for agent-agnostic safety guarantees in environments that can only be partially observed and within which an action is required within limited time. We introduce two realistic scenarios, one with an infinite state space and one with signal mixture. In these scenarios, we prove that even a perfect agent cannot guarantee safe behaviour. It will be argued that for any proof of AI safety or alignment, the environment and associated safe actions need to be specifically considered together with the agent.

</details>


### [323] [The Handover Problem: Governing Autonomy Transitions in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10352)
*Vicente Pelechano,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"交接问题"（Handover Problem），将AI自主性级别的调整形式化为可审计的治理决策，并引入交接就绪分数（HRS）与迟滞转换策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机协作研究（自适应自动化、监督控制、自动化信任、去技能化）虽各自解释了部分现象，但缺乏跨多周期工作流的、可审计的多信号判据来决定何时应改变AI自主性级别。

Method: 形式化"交接问题"：在每个操作周期决定升级、维持或回退AI自主性，并保证过程可逆、可恢复、可审计。提出HRS复合指标，融合操作员就绪度、人机信任、学习稳定性、操作绩效四个信号维度；结合基于迟滞的转换策略，要求持续正向证据才提升自主性，条件恶化时迅速回退。

Result: 在软件工程与制造领域，HRS与硬性安全防护互补：防护机制在单一指标突破关键阈值时触发即时纠正，HRS则能捕捉无单一防护可观测的操作员就绪度缓慢、多信号侵蚀。

Conclusion: 该框架将自主性交接确立为需要明确、复合且可审计判据的治理问题，为自适应自动化研究提供了此前缺失的概念与形式化基础。

Abstract: Human-machine systems rarely operate at a fixed level of AI autonomy. As operators and AI systems collaborate over time, control must shift: the AI can take on more responsibility when collaboration is stable, maintain its current role when evidence is ambiguous, or return control to the human when conditions deteriorate. Existing work on adaptive automation, supervisory control, trust in automation, and deskilling explains parts of this problem, but provides no auditable, multi-signal criterion for governing when autonomy should change across multi-cycle workflows.
  We formalise this challenge as the Handover Problem: deciding, at each operational cycle, whether to escalate, maintain, or revert AI autonomy while keeping the process reversible, recoverable, and auditable. We introduce the Handover Readiness Score (HRS), a transparent composite measure that integrates four signal dimensions: operator readiness, human-AI trust, learning stability, and operational performance. It is combined with a hysteresis-based transition policy that requires sustained positive evidence before increasing autonomy but reverts promptly when conditions worsen.
  Across software engineering and manufacturing domains, the HRS and hard safety guards address complementary failure regimes: guards enforce immediate corrective action when a single indicator breaches a critical threshold, while the HRS detects the slow, multi-signal erosion of operator readiness that no individual guard can observe. The framework establishes autonomy handover as a governance problem requiring explicit, composite, and auditable criteria. This provides a conceptual and formal foundation that adaptive automation research has not previously provided.

</details>


### [324] [Open-MMUnlearning: Unifying Methods and Evaluation for MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10358)
*Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Qianshan Wei,Junxiang You,Jingwen Shao,Junkai Lin,Zhongkai Yue,Xiaotian Ye,Zhengbo Jiao,Jiali Cheng,Zhijie Deng,Kening Zheng,Ruiqi Liu,Hadi Amiri,Yi Yu,Zhenan Sun,Qi Li,Ka-Ho Chow,Sijia Liu,Liang Wang,Jiaqi Li,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开源可扩展框架 Open-MMUnlearning，统一 MLLM 遗忘的目标模型准备、多模态数据处理、遗忘与评估流程，并系统比较十种遗忘方法与十三种评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型能力增强与部署扩大，隐私与安全担忧日益突出。机器遗忘可在保留无关能力的同时移除指定信息，但现有实现与评估协议碎片化、鲁棒性测试不完整、对指标可靠性理解有限，导致 MLLM 遗忘研究进展难以被系统评估。

Method: 构建 Open-MMUnlearning 框架，通过共享接口与结构化配置整合目标模型准备、多模态数据处理、遗忘与评估；覆盖隐私、安全、版权五类基准，四个模型家族八种 MLLM，十二种遗忘方法；评估套件联合考察遗忘有效性、保留效用及对模型干预、对抗输入与成员推断攻击的鲁棒性；在统一协议下比较十种代表性遗忘方法；并提出指标元评估协议，用对目标知识有受控暴露的模型检验忠实性，并在量化与重学习条件下检验鲁棒性。

Result: GD 与 MIP-Editor 总得分并列最高：GD 遗忘质量最高，MIP-Editor 更好保留模型效用。十三种被评估指标中，BLEU 综合可靠性得分最高；KS-Test 忠实性 AUC 最高，但鲁棒性表现较弱。

Conclusion: 该框架与发现共同支撑 MLLM 遗忘方法的可复现比较与评估可靠性的系统评估。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) become more capable and widely deployed, concerns about privacy and safety have become increasingly pressing. Machine unlearning offers one approach to addressing these concerns by removing designated information from trained models while preserving unrelated capabilities. However, fragmented implementations and evaluation protocols, incomplete robustness testing, and limited understanding of metric reliability make progress in MLLM unlearning difficult to assess systematically. We introduce Open-MMUnlearning, an open-source, extensible framework that integrates target-model preparation, multimodal data processing, unlearning, and evaluation through shared interfaces and structured configurations. The framework supports five benchmarks spanning privacy, safety, and copyright, eight MLLMs from four model families, and twelve unlearning methods. Its evaluation suite jointly assesses forgetting effectiveness, retained utility, and robustness to model interventions, adversarial inputs, and membership inference attacks. Using a common evaluation protocol, we compare ten representative unlearning methods. In this comparison, GD and MIP-Editor tie for the highest overall score: GD achieves the highest Forget Quality, while MIP-Editor preserves more Model Utility. We further introduce a metric meta-evaluation protocol that tests faithfulness using models with controlled exposure to target knowledge and robustness under quantization and relearning. Among the thirteen evaluated metrics, BLEU achieves the highest aggregate reliability score. KS-Test attains the highest faithfulness AUC but performs less well on robustness. Together, the framework and these findings support reproducible comparison of MLLM unlearning methods and systematic assessment of evaluation reliability.

</details>


### [325] [SOTA: Stock Options Trading Agents Guided by Option-Implied Return Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10407)
*Yizhen Xie,Mengyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SOTA 期权交易智能体框架，将庞大期权合约空间抽象为策略级决策，由确定性解析器实现组合，经 SFT+RL 后训练，在半年样本外取得 18.3% 收益、Sharpe 1.60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期权市场增长与 AI 进步推动智能体交易研究，但期权交易决策极难：单只股票可有数千合约，需同时决定交易哪些合约及如何组合；现有方法多以固定策略结构（如跨式）限制策略空间，无法随市场变化切换策略。

Method: 提出 SOTA（Stock Options Trading Agents）框架，把巨大期权全集抽象为策略层面决策，组合实现交由确定性解析器完成；以 Qwen3-27B 为基座，经监督微调（SFT）后接强化学习（RL）进行后训练。

Result: 在九只美股大盘股及 SPY 期权上，与同一交易环境中的规则型和机器学习策略选择器对比，六个月样本外总收益 18.3%，Sharpe 比率 1.60，最大回撤 8.96%。并发现新闻存在非对称作用：新闻可提升前沿教师轨迹质量，但在 RL 阶段保留新闻会使样本外收益从 18.3% 降至 -2.7%。

Conclusion: 策略级抽象加确定性执行是应对期权大决策空间的有效途径，后训练语言模型智能体可超越规则与机器学习选择器；新闻信息的使用需区分阶段，训练信号与策略优化阶段的信息配置不当会显著损害泛化表现。

Abstract: As option markets grow and AI advances, agentic systems for option trading are gaining increasing attention. Language-model-based agents can reason over contextual information such as news, but option trading presents a particularly challenging decision problem: a single stock can have thousands of contracts, and the agent must decide both which contracts to trade and how to combine them. Existing approaches often sidestep this complexity by restricting the policy to a fixed strategy structure, such as a straddle, limiting their ability to switch strategies as market conditions change. We present SOTA (Stock Options Trading Agents), an agentic trading framework for structured option-strategy selection. SOTA abstracts the large option universe into strategy-level decisions while deterministic resolvers handle portfolio implementation. We develop SOTA by post-training Qwen3.8-27B with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. SOTA is evaluated on options on nine large-cap U.S. equities and SPY against rule-based and machine-learning strategy selectors in the same trading environment. Over a six-month out-of-sample period, SOTA earns an 18.3% total return with a Sharpe ratio of 1.60 and a maximum drawdown of 8.96%. We also document an asymmetric role of news: news improves frontier-teacher trajectories, but retaining news during reinforcement learning reduces out-of-sample return from 18.3% to -2.7%.

</details>


### [326] [Before They Can Solve: Predicting Post-Training Coding-Agent Performance from Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478)
*Tan Yu,Alexander Bukharin,Khushi Bhardwaj,Jennifer Williams,Zirui Liu,Jonathan Lingjie Li,Soumye Singhal,Joseph Jennings,Sanjeev Satheesh,Yash Jain,Ashish Vaswani,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Matthew Papakipos,Hyunwoo Kim,Jian Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Markus Kliegl,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bryan Catanzaro,Jonathan Cohen,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用"成功后训练轨迹的决断步"作为基座模型潜力的前瞻信号，并设计三种无需基座模型冷启动驱动harness的筛选方法，在十个基座/后训练模型对上与后训练 SWE-bench Verified pass@1 排名高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测哪个基座检查点值得进行昂贵的agentic后训练是一个关键但困难的问题。端到端 pass@K 虽能检验成功行为是否已出现在基座分布中，却不适合 agentic coding：许多基座模型无法可靠产生完成任务所需的规范工具调用。单步或短程任务虽规避了工具调用失败，却把多步交互压缩成固定提示与单一补丁，绕开了真正关心的核心能力——在仓库持续演化中维持多步工具使用的连贯状态。

Method: 将成功的后训练 agent 轨迹视为基座模型潜力的前瞻信号。通过回放每条轨迹并在每个改动代码的步骤后重跑测试，识别出"决断步"：即累积补丁首次使仓库由失败转为通过的步骤，证明该动作在先前上下文下可解任务。基于 agentic 迹的覆盖原则，在此步骤构建三种无需基座检查点从冷启动驱动 harness 的筛选：(i) Decisive-Action BPB（每字节比特）度量认证动作上的概率质量；(ii) Patch MCQ 测试检查点在该动作与同一验证器拒绝的备选之间的选择；(iii) prefix-conditioned pass@K 评估对功能正确生成的支持，并计入测试接受的任意续写。

Result: 在十对公开的基座与后训练模型上，三种筛选方法对模型队列的排名与后训练 SWE-bench Verified pass@1 高度一致。由于方法仅需基准的成功轨迹与验证器，可推广用于将未来 agentic coding 基准转化为基座模型评测。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于决断步的三种筛选方法可在不依赖基座模型冷启动驱动 harness 的前提下，有效预测基座检查点经 agentic 后训练的潜力，并与实际后训练性能排名高度吻合，为将 agentic coding 基准改造为基座模型评测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: How can we predict which base checkpoint is worth an expensive round of agentic post-training? End-to-end pass@$K$ tests whether successful behavior already appears in a base model's distribution, but it is a poor fit for agentic coding: many base checkpoints cannot reliably produce the well-formed tool invocation required to complete a task end-to-end. Single-shot or short-horizon tasks avoid these tool-calling failures by collapsing a multi-step interaction into a fixed prompt and a single patch, but they sidestep the core capability we care about: maintaining coherent state over many tool-using steps as the repository evolves. To bridge this gap, we treat successful post-trained agent trajectories as a lookahead signal of base-model potential. Replaying each trajectory and rerunning tests after every code-changing step identifies the decisive step: the first step whose cumulative patch flips the repository from failing to passing, certifying that the recorded action solves the task given the prior context. Motivated by a coverage principle for agentic traces, we build three screens at this step that do not require a base checkpoint to drive the harness from a cold start: (i) Decisive-Action BPB (bits per byte) measures the probability mass on the certified action, (ii) Patch MCQ tests the checkpoint's choice between that action and alternatives rejected by the same verifier, and (iii) prefix-conditioned pass@$K$ evaluates support for functionally-correct generations and credits any continuation that the tests accept. Across ten pairs of public base and post-trained models, all three screens rank the cohort in close agreement with post-trained SWE-bench Verified pass@$1$. As our methods need only a benchmark's successful trajectories and its verifier, they can be applied to turn future agentic coding benchmarks into base-model evaluations.

</details>


### [327] [EmbodiedRSI: Active Continual Robot Learning Through Hypothesis-Guided Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10498)
*Python Song,Zhixuan Liang,Kelsey Fu,Mengdi Wang,Junfeng Yang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmbodiedRSI：一种自进化智能体框架，通过快慢双系统、假设图与信息价值实验选择，实现代码-技能协同进化与记忆学习，在RoboCasa365、LIBERO-Pro及真实机器人上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型虽具备较强视觉运动控制能力，但当物体位置或任务指令变化时性能会下降。现有改进方式通常依赖昂贵的后训练（如遥操作采集机器人数据），而当前自进化智能体框架在决策探索哪些代码与技能变更时，对机器人试次的利用效率低下。

Method: 提出EmbodiedRSI自进化智能体框架。核心为快慢双系统架构：在假设图中维护相互竞争的代码与技能假设；采用信息价值实验选择（Value-of-Information Experiment Selection）挑选能区分假设的物理实验，其结果驱动代码-技能协同进化（Code-Skill Co-Evolution）；慢系统构建分层记忆（Hierarchical Memory），并通过奖励锚定记忆学习（Reward-Grounded Memory Learning）按对快系统后续改进的价值筛选有效记忆。

Result: 在RoboCasa365上整体成功率77.0%，Composite-Unseen上71.3%，而最佳基线仅40.1%；在LIBERO-Pro上整体成功率86.8%。此外，EmbodiedRSI可零样本迁移至真实机器人，在多项挑战性任务上整体成功率71.3%。

Conclusion: EmbodiedRSI通过自主决定探索方向并将物理交互转化为代码与技能的持续改进，显著提升机器人基础模型在分布变化下的适应能力，并具备从仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Robot foundation models provide strong visuomotor control, yet their performance can degrade when object positions or task instructions change. Further improvements often require post-training on substantial robot data, which can be costly to collect through methods such as teleoperation. Agentic harnesses can adapt around the model, but current self-evolving harnesses use robot trials inefficiently when deciding which code and skill changes to pursue. We introduce EmbodiedRSI, a self-evolving agentic harness that autonomously decides where to explore next and turns the resulting physical interaction into improved code and skills. EmbodiedRSI realizes this through a Fast-Slow Dual-System Architecture, in which competing code and skill hypotheses are maintained in a Hypothesis Graph. Value-of-Information Experiment Selection chooses physical experiments that can distinguish these hypotheses. Their outcomes guide Code-Skill Co-Evolution. The Slow System builds Hierarchical Memory, and Reward-Grounded Memory Learning selects effective memory according to their value for later Fast-System improvement. On RoboCasa365, EmbodiedRSI reaches 77.0% overall success and 71.3% on Composite-Unseen, compared with 40.1% for the best baseline. EmbodiedRSI also reaches 86.8% overall success on LIBERO-Pro. Beyond benchmark performance, EmbodiedRSI transfers zero-shot to real-world robot, achieving 71.3% overall success across multiple challenging tasks.

</details>


### [328] [RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507)
*Yilun Hao,Krishna Sayana,Isabella Ye,James S Ren,Sukhdeep Sodhi,Craig Boutilier,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECAST 将证据构建建模为对异构检索与计算操作的序贯决策过程，使证据可被主动推导而非仅检索，在六个基准上平均成功率 75.6%，超越最强基线 15.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 RAG 依赖固定的相似度检索，智能体式变体虽能调整查询与工具使用，但仍以检索为中心。然而许多任务所需的证据并不显式存在于任何单一源条目中，必须通过跨多个条目的过滤、聚合或计算推导得到，现有范式无法满足这一需求。

Method: 提出 RECAST 学习框架，将证据构建形式化为对异构检索与计算操作的序贯决策过程。轻量 RouterLM 迭代地选择并表述原语操作，或为冻结的 CompilerLM 指定定制操作以翻译为可执行代码；当判定证据充分后，RouterLM 将已验证证据传递给冻结的 AnswerLM 生成最终答案。训练采用监督微调（SFT）加组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在六个异构基准族上平均成功率达 75.6%，较最强的大模型基线高出 15.9%；经训练后 Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM 比免训练的 Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM 高 5.0%。在三个留出基准上平均较最强基线提升 15.0%，展现出跨任务与异构源表示的强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 证据构建应从"检索"转向"推导"，通过对检索与计算操作的序贯决策可有效应对长、异构信息源任务；轻量可训练路由器配合冻结的编译与回答模型即可取得显著性能提升与良好泛化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively derived rather than merely retrieved. A lightweight RouterLM iteratively selects and formulates primitive operations or specifies customized operations for a frozen CompilerLM to translate into executable code. Once it judges the evidence sufficient, RouterLM passes the accepted evidence to a frozen AnswerLM to produce the final solution. We train RouterLM with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across six heterogeneous benchmark families, RECAST achieves a mean success rate of 75.6%, outperforming the strongest large-model baseline by 15.9%. Moreover, training enables the Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM to outperform a training-free Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM by 5.0%. On three held-out benchmarks, RECAST improves over the strongest baseline by 15.0% on average, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization across tasks and heterogeneous source representations.

</details>


### [329] [SciExam for ENSO: Can AI Agents Build Climate Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513)
*Yinling Zhang,Langchen Liu,Dongbin Xiu,Xueyan Zou,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciExam for ENSO是一个让语言模型智能体依据真实观测数据、在六小时内自主构建ENSO低阶随机模型的基准，隐藏评分者从统计重现、隐变量恢复与预测三方面评估模型。十二个智能体中六个优于已发表模型，其简化形式还与ENSO冷暖不对称的两大竞争解释之一相容，表明该基准可在无已知答案的情形下评估AI科研能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对语言模型智能体科研能力的评估多依赖已知答案、评分细则或语言模型评审，这些方式都无法判断一个新的科学模型是否有效，因而需要一种在"无标准答案"情形下检验智能体科研产出的评估范式。

Method: 提出SciExam for ENSO基准：智能体在六小时内处理真实ENSO观测数据，自行编写诊断程序（随后被冻结），并仅以这些诊断为反馈来构建低阶随机模型；隐藏评分者随后检验模型能否重现ENSO统计特征、恢复未观测变量、预报留出年份，并以同样方式对一篇已发表模型打分。此外还设置受控实验，改变智能体可获得的信息以考察信息来源。

Result: 在十二个智能体系统中，六个构建的模型得分高于已发表模型，主要优势体现在重建与预报上；较强模型的简化形式各自与ENSO冷暖不对称两种竞争解释之一相容，而该开放争论并未在任务中被提及。对最优系统的受控运行显示，其得分并非来自对陈旧观测记录的回忆，且所获信息会塑造其建模方式。

Conclusion: SciExam for ENSO证明可在答案未知的情形下评估智能体的科研能力；结果表明智能体已能构建有竞争力的模型，且其结构与科学家仍在争论的问题相关。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out open-ended scientific research, yet their results are usually graded against a known answer, a rubric, or a language-model reviewer, none of which can tell whether a new scientific model is valid. The AI Science Exam for El Nino-Southern Oscillation (SciExam for ENSO) is a benchmark in which agents build low-order stochastic models of ENSO, the dominant mode of interannual climate variability, from real observations. Within a six-hour budget, agents process the observations, write their own diagnostics, which are then frozen, and develop a model using only these diagnostics as feedback. Hidden graders then test whether the model reproduces ENSO's statistics, recovers unobserved variables, and forecasts held-out years, and score a published model in the same way. Across twelve agent systems, six produce models that score higher than the published model, mainly through better reconstruction and forecasting. The simplified forms of the stronger models are each compatible with one of the two competing explanations of ENSO's warm-cold asymmetry, an open debate that the task never mentions. Controlled runs of the top system under varied information suggest that its scores do not come from recalling the dated observational record and that the information it receives shapes how it builds its model. SciExam for ENSO can thus evaluate agent research where no answer is known, and the results suggest that agents can already build competitive models whose structures bear on questions that scientists still debate.

</details>


### [330] [RoboJEPA: Scaling Robotic Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10515)
*Artem Zholus,Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Jianhao Yuan,Sarath Chandar,Tushar Nagarajan,Daniel Severo,Koustuv Sinha,Michal Drozdzal,Adriana Romero Soriano,Jeannette Bohg,Nicolas Ballas,Mahmoud Assran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboJEPA，一个基于 JEPA 架构、在覆盖 12 种机器人本体的数据集上训练的世界模型；首次为多本体机器人世界模型建立缩放律，并展示其可零样本部署于真实硬件完成长时程规划任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型虽能预测未来状态并用于规划，但缺乏原则性方法估计其能力如何随模型规模、数据与算力扩展，这一开放问题拖慢了领域进展；同时需要可靠替代真实机器人评测的代理指标。

Method: 基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）构建 RoboJEPA，在横跨 12 种机器人本体的大规模数据集上训练，参数规模达 8B（迄今最大 JEPA 预测器模型）；以“想象误差”（潜在展开误差）为度量，拟合并分析其与算力的缩放关系，并考察下游规划性能与误差的相关性；将模型零样本部署为机器人智能体，以单张目标图像为引导进行规划。

Result: 想象误差随算力呈二阶幂律下降，可用于预测远超拟合规模之外的模型质量；下游机器人规划性能随算力可预测地提升，且与想象误差强相关，说明想象误差是真实机器人评测的可靠代理；模型可零样本作为机器人智能体部署，在真实硬件上完成需要长时程规划的任务。所有模型检查点及训练与部署代码均已开源。

Conclusion: 本文首次为基于真实机器人数据训练的多本体机器人世界模型建立缩放律，验证了缩放律对规划能力的预测性与想象误差作为评测代理的有效性，并展示了潜在世界模型零样本真实部署的可行性。

Abstract: Latent world models have shown a remarkable ability to predict future states and to plan in the real world. In practice, however, we lack a principled way to estimate how their capabilities scale with model size, data, and compute, an open problem that slows progress in the field. In this work we present RoboJEPA, a world model based on the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and trained on a large-scale dataset spanning 12 robotic embodiments. We show that RoboJEPA's imagination error, the error of its latent rollouts, follows a second-order power law in compute, allowing us to predict model quality well beyond the scale at which the law is fit. We further show that downstream robotic planning performance improves predictably with compute, and that imagination error is strongly correlated with it, making it a reliable proxy for real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate that latent world models can be deployed zero-shot as robotic agents, planning toward a single goal image to solve tasks requiring long-horizon planning on real hardware. We release all model checkpoints together with our training and robot deployment code. To our knowledge, this is the first work to establish scaling laws for multi-embodiment robotic world models trained on real robot data, and RoboJEPA, at 8B parameters, is the largest JEPA predictor model trained to date.

</details>


### [331] [Efficient Dense Crowd Trajectory Prediction Via Dynamic Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18166)
*Antonius Bima Murti Wijaya,Paul Henderson,Marwa Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于聚类的即插即用人群轨迹预测方法，通过按相似属性随时间对个体分组并以其质心替代行人输入，在保持精度的同时实现更快处理与更低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群轨迹预测对公共安全与管理至关重要，可用于预防踩踏等灾害。现有工作多基于人工标注数据预测个体轨迹并考虑周围对象，但往往忽视密集人群场景；而密集场景下跟踪输出规模大、噪声多、不准确，导致自动化挑战加剧且计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于聚类的即插即用（plug-and-play）方法：随时间将具有相似属性的个体聚为一组，通过准确的群体摘要（group summarisation）加速执行；并将其输出的聚类质心（centroid）替代原行人输入，从而与现有轨迹预测器结合使用。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的密集人群场景上进行了评估，结果表明：相较于当前最先进方法，本方法处理速度更快、内存占用更低，同时保持预测精度。

Conclusion: 基于聚类的群体摘要可有效缓解密集人群轨迹预测中因跟踪输出庞大、噪声与不准确带来的计算负担，作为即插即用模块兼顾效率与精度。

Abstract: Crowd trajectory prediction plays a crucial role in public safety and management, where it can help prevent disasters such as stampedes. Recent works address the problem by predicting individual trajectories and considering surrounding objects based on manually annotated data. However, these approaches tend to overlook dense crowd scenarios, where the challenges of automation become more pronounced due to the massiveness, noisiness, and inaccuracy of the tracking outputs, resulting in high computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose and extensively evaluate a novel cluster-based approach that groups individuals based on similar attributes over time, enabling faster execution through accurate group summarisation. Our plug-and-play method can be combined with existing trajectory predictors by using our output centroid in place of their pedestrian input. We evaluate our proposed method on several challenging dense crowd scenes. We demonstrated that our approach leads to faster processing and lower memory usage when compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the accuracy

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [Physics-based Sphere Packing for Lagrangian Mesh Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08880)
*Jiong Lin,Hod Lipson*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出JamTet，一种基于球堆积的体网格生成与形变框架，包含GPU并行四面体网格化、保持节点对应关系的拉格朗日形变与JAX可微GPU模拟器，在软体机器人形态设计中验证内部节点梯度对适应度的提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体网格是可微模拟与计算设计的常用形体表示。现有方法存在两难：固定连接网格在大幅形变下退化（甚至反转），而从头重新划分网格则丢失节点对应关系，导致无法在梯度优化中保持一致的参数化。

Method: （1）GPU并行网格化器：将八叉树分层球堆积与约束Delaunay四面体化结合，单元体积均匀性优于TetGen与fTetWild；（2）拉格朗日网格形变：在变化形状中重新平衡同一组球、重建边界与连接关系，从而保留内部节点身份，在固定连接网格与TetSphere网格发生反转的场景下保持无反转；（3）JAX实现的可微GPU模拟器，含质点-弹簧边与体积Neo-Hookean项，并与网格形变集成于设计流程。

Result: 在软体机器人形态设计实验中，包含内部节点梯度的方案相比仅表面梯度变体，游泳适应度提升0.73-1.07；相同设计的体素化版本适应度低32-63%。网格化质量上，单元体积比TetGen与fTetWild更均匀。

Conclusion: 结果表明球堆积可作为一种实用的体网格表示，适用于基于梯度的形状优化，兼顾形变鲁棒性、节点对应关系与可微性。

Abstract: This paper studies tetrahedral meshes as the body representation for differentiable simulation and computational design. Fixed-connectivity meshes degrade under large morphs, while remeshing from scratch discards node correspondence. We present JamTet, a physics-based sphere-packing framework for volumetric meshing and morphing. We contribute (i) a GPU-parallel mesher combining octree-hierarchical packing with constrained Delaunay tetrahedralization, producing more uniform element volumes than TetGen and fTetWild; (ii) Lagrangian mesh morphing that preserves interior-node identities by re-equilibrating the same spheres within changing shapes and rebuilding the boundary and connectivity, remaining inversion-free where fixed-connectivity and TetSphere meshes invert; and (iii) a differentiable GPU simulator in JAX, with mass-spring edges and a volumetric Neo-Hookean term, integrated with mesh morphing in a design pipeline. In soft-robot morphology design experiments, interior-node gradients improve swimming fitness by 0.73-1.07 over a matched surface-only variant, while voxelized versions of the same designs yield 32-63% lower fitness. These results establish sphere packing as a practical volumetric mesh representation for gradient-based shape optimization. Code and media: under review.

</details>


### [333] [emg2face: Expressive Facial Animation with High-Density Surface EMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09304)
*Ganidhu Abey,Wendy Greening,Ashika Kamboj,Leonhard Helminger,Abhijeet Ghosh,Karel Petranek,Sergio Orts Escolano,Dinesh K. Pai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出高密度表面肌电（HD-sEMG）作为面部动作捕获的非光学替代方案，通过64通道织物EMG网格采集信号，训练深度网络从EMG预测表情blendshape参数，实现遮挡场景下的面部表情重建与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学面部捕获方法在头戴设备（HMD，如VR头显）遮挡面部时难以或无法使用，即使视线通畅也存在隐私问题，且需要将摄像头和照明从头面部偏移安装的头戴式采集装置，因此需要一种非光学的替代方案。

Method: 采用两块织物EMG网格采集64通道EMG（前额32通道+面侧32通道），2048 Hz采样并滤波；同步用MediaPipe Face Landmarker估计478个3D面部landmark；提出基于模拟音频脉冲的亚毫秒级同步方法解决EMG与视频异频独立时钟的同步问题；提出分阶段拟合方法将高分辨率参数化头模型GNM（253 identity blendshapes + 383 expression blendshapes）拟合到landmark；训练深度神经网络（每网格空间编码器 + 膨胀时间卷积网络TCN），从HD-sEMG以100 Hz预测blendshape参数。

Result: 在25名参与者的记录上验证了方法，训练后的网络仅凭HD-sEMG即可预测表情blendshape，输出可通过标准实时blendshape动画方法渲染，并实现表情向多种人脸及非人类角色的直接迁移。

Conclusion: HD-sEMG为应对HMD遮挡、隐私等光学捕获挑战提供了可行的非光学替代方案，能够实现遮挡区域的面部表情捕获、参数化重建与跨角色表情迁移。

Abstract: Facial movements convey subtle and important information that is critical for human social communication. Optical methods for face capture are difficult or impossible to use when the face is occluded by head-mounted devices (HMDs), such as VR headsets. Even with a clear line of sight, such methods raise privacy concerns and require head-mounted capture rigs that offset cameras and lighting from the face. We show that high-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG) provides a viable non-optical alternative that addresses these challenges.
  We measured 64 EMG channels, using two textile EMG grids, with 32 from the forehead (typically occluded by an HMD) and 32 from the side of the face. EMG data were digitized at 2048 Hz and filtered. Facial movements were simultaneously recorded and used to estimate 478 3D facial landmarks using MediaPipe's Face Landmarker. A major challenge in such multimodal recordings is synchronizing EMG and video data, which have different sampling frequencies and independent clocks. We developed a novel synchronization method using analog audio bursts that is capable of sub-millisecond synchronization. We also developed a staged fitting method that fits a recent high-resolution parametric head model (GNM), with 253 identity blendshapes and 383 expression blendshapes, to the MediaPipe landmarks as participants performed different facial expressions.
  We trained a deep neural network comprising per-grid spatial encoders followed by a dilated temporal convolutional network (TCN) to predict blendshape parameters from HD-sEMG signals at 100 Hz. Once trained, the network can predict expression blendshapes solely from HD-sEMG recordings. The output can be rendered using standard real-time blendshape animation methods. We demonstrate the methods using recordings from 25 participants, and direct expression transfer to a variety of human faces and non-human characters.

</details>


### [334] [ScribbleEdit: A Benchmark for Scribble-Only Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09382)
*Jie Ren,Hao Kang,Kai Guo,Yiding Yang,Bo Liu,Liming Jiang,Qing Yan,Zichuan Liu,Yizhi Song,Yue Xing,Hui Liu,Xin Lu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ScribbleEdit 基准，系统评估图像编辑模型理解涂鸦输入意图并执行编辑的能力，发现现有 VLM/LLM 模型难以准确捕捉涂鸦意图，并提出一种简单有效的 soft-token 基线方法改善该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于涂鸦的交互方式轻量直观，用户可借此指定编辑意图，但当前基于 VLM 或 LLM 的图像编辑模型难以仅凭涂鸦输入理解和执行编辑。为系统研究该问题，作者构建了新基准。

Method: 构建 ScribbleEdit 基准，要求模型同时深入理解涂鸦意图并准确解析其空间信息；设计自动化数据构建流程，以及显式度量意图对齐的专用评估协议；提出一种 soft-token 基线方法，增强模型对涂鸦语义的理解。

Result: 分析表明现有基于 VLM/LLM 的编辑模型无法准确捕捉涂鸦意图；所提 soft-token 基线在 ScribbleEdit 基准上优于标准图像编辑模型。

Conclusion: 该评估体系与基线方法共同为评估与改进涂鸦驱动的图像编辑提供了具体基础，可指导未来仅基于涂鸦的图像编辑研究。

Abstract: Scribble-based interaction provides a lightweight and intuitive way for users to specify image editing intents in interactive editing tools. However, current image editing models based on VLMs or LLMs struggle to understand and execute edits based solely on scribble inputs. To systematically study this problem, we construct a new benchmark, ScribbleEdit, that evaluates the ability of image editing models to perform image editing conditioned on scribbles. This task requires both a deep understanding of the intention of the scribble and an accurate interpretation of its spatial information. In ScribbleEdit, we design an automated data construction pipeline and introduce a dedicated evaluation protocol that explicitly measures intention alignment. Our analysis reveals that existing VLM/LLM-based editing models fail to accurately capture scribble intentions. To guide future progress on scribble-only image editing, we propose a simple yet effective soft-token baseline, which enhances the model's understanding of scribble semantics and outperforms standard image editing models on our benchmark. Our evaluation and baseline together provide a concrete foundation for assessing and improving the scribble-driven image editing.

</details>


### [335] [Simulation Methods for Multiphysics Phenomena in Visual Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09822)
*Fabian Löschner,Stefan Rhys Jeske,José Antonio Fernández-Fernández,Jan Bender*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 这是一份面向计算机图形学的多物理仿真课程笔记，系统介绍刚体、可变形体、流体、颗粒材料及其耦合的数学基础、算法与实践，并涵盖软件框架与机器学习趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真是游戏、虚拟现实、视觉特效与计算设计等图形学应用的核心。过去几十年间，基于物理的建模与动画技术数量激增，可模拟的材料与物理现象日益丰富，因此需要一份对主流多物理仿真方法的深入导引。

Method: 以课程笔记形式，围绕图形学界发展的多物理仿真方法展开：对每种方法给出底层数学框架与详细推导，讨论不同材料与耦合策略如何纳入该形式化体系；覆盖刚体、可变形体、流体、颗粒材料及其相互作用。

Result: 提供了多物理仿真方法的系统性梳理，包括数学基础、关键算法与实践考量；同时给出若干可直接使用的多物理建模软件框架，并讨论了基于机器学习的物理动画等新兴趋势。

Conclusion: 该笔记为图形学多物理仿真提供了从理论推导到工程实践的完整参考，并指出机器学习方法正日益流行，是未来物理动画的重要方向。

Abstract: Physics simulation is a cornerstone of many computer graphics applications, ranging from video games and virtual reality to visual effects and computational design. The number of techniques for physically-based modeling and animation has thus skyrocketed over the past few decades, facilitating the simulation of a wide variety of materials and physical phenomena. These course notes provide an in-depth introduction to multiphysics simulation methods for computer graphics, covering the mathematical foundations, key algorithms, and practical considerations behind the most widely used approaches. We focus on methods developed by the computer graphics community for simulating various physical phenomena and materials -- including rigid and deformable bodies, fluids, and granular materials -- as well as the interactions between them. For each method, we present the underlying mathematical framework with detailed derivations and discuss how different materials and coupling strategies fit into the formulation. A selection of software frameworks that offer out-of-the-box multiphysics modeling capabilities is also presented. Finally, we touch on emerging trends in physics-based animation, including machine learning-based methods which have become increasingly popular in recent years.

</details>


### [336] [DynaConTalk: Wavelet-Constrained Diffusion for Long-Form and Controllable Holistic Co-Speech 3D Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09846)
*Yifei Zhu,Yangyang Cai,Mingyi Shi,Miao Cheng,Lin Gu,Taku Komura,Yoshifumi Kitamura*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出DynaConTalk，一个基于小波约束扩散的框架，用于生成长时长、可控的整体协同语音动作，通过在小波变换系数空间中进行扩散并动态融合多模态条件来缓解动作平滑与条件平均化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体协同语音动画在动作表示与语音条件两方面容易趋于平均：坐标空间扩散将慢速身体姿态、中频手势笔画与快速手部/面部细节纠缠在单一预测目标中，常导致低方差、过度平滑的动作；同时固定多模态融合下，密集的节奏与声学线索会压制稀疏的内容特定信息。

Method: 提出DynaConTalk小波约束扩散框架：在平稳小波变换(SWT)系数空间进行扩散，其时间对齐频带分离粗姿态演化、手势笔画与精细表情细节；动态门控网络以HuBERT与说话人身份为基础，通过动作状态与噪声感知的残差门选择性加入节奏、梅尔与文本特征；注意力池化与学习式深度路由为每个去噪阶段提供互补条件，帧分辨率节奏路径保留精确时序；签名提案-共识更新协调条件与演化动作状态；匹配噪声约束注入统一用于历史延续与局部关键姿势修复，并扩展至参考引导控制；分离的身体-手部与面部去噪器，经逆SWT与姿态驱动根回归器生成整体动作。

Result: 实验评估了生成质量、面部准确性、时间连续性与可控编辑，代码、模型与交互式编辑界面已开源。

Conclusion: DynaConTalk通过小波域扩散与动态多模态条件融合，有效缓解动作平滑与条件平均化，实现了长时长、可控的整体协同语音动作生成。

Abstract: Holistic co-speech animation is prone to averaging in both motion representation and speech conditioning. In coordinate-space diffusion, slow body posture, mid-frequency gesture strokes, and fast hand or facial details are entangled in one prediction target, often producing low-variance, over-smoothed motion. Meanwhile, dense rhythmic and acoustic cues can dominate sparse content-specific information under fixed multimodal fusion. We present DynaConTalk, a wavelet-constrained diffusion framework for long-form and controllable holistic co-speech motion generation. Diffusion operates in stationary wavelet transform (SWT) coefficient space, whose temporally aligned bands separate coarse posture evolution, gesture strokes, and fine expressive details. Our dynamic gating network preserves HuBERT and speaker identity as a base and selectively adds rhythm, mel, and transcript features through motion-state- and noise-aware residual gates. Attention pooling and learned depth routing deliver complementary conditions to each denoising stage, while a frame-resolution rhythm path preserves precise timing. A signed proposal-consensus update then reconciles these conditions with the evolving motion state. Matched-noise constraint injection uses the same sampling interface for history continuation and localized keypose repair, and extends to reference-guided control. Separate body-hand and facial denoisers, followed by inverse SWT and a pose-driven root regressor, produce holistic motion. Experiments evaluate generation quality, facial accuracy, temporal continuity, and controllable editing. Code, models, and the interactive editing interface are available at https://github.com/zhuyifeiabcd1/DynaConTalk.

</details>


### [337] [TRACK: Telemetry-Based Racing Analysis and Coaching Kit in Sim Racing Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10061)
*Efe Çangırılı,Murat Kurt*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACK框架，将每个驾驶会话表示为速度、制动、策略、一致性四维行为空间中的几何指纹，并用无监督聚类按相似性分组，同时以强化学习参考智能体归一化指标，报告其在ACGym数据集上的初步结果与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim racing分析多局限于给车手打分或套用预设标签，缺乏对车手驾驶行为本身的可解释刻画；作者希望建立个性化驾驶改进建议系统的分析基础，因此需要一个能够刻画驾驶行为、且不依赖样本的评分尺度。

Method: 构建TRACK（Telemetry-Based Racing Analysis and Coaching Kit）框架：将每段录制会话表示为速度、制动、策略、一致性四维行为空间中的紧凑几何指纹，用无监督聚类按相似性分组；每个聚类结果都与零假设（null）校准，无法显著区分于随机时如实报告；所有指标相对一个强化学习参考智能体进行归一化，使量纲不随样本变化而漂移。研究在开放的Assetto Corsa Gym（ACGym）数据集上迭代开发与细化。

Result: 发现弯道类型在定义它们时未使用的某个行为维度上也存在差异；在受限样本中更换车辆后，仅速度与一致性具有可迁移性，制动与策略指标的可重复性未能得到证明。随着可用遥测范围扩大，聚类分离度下降。此外，尚不清楚车手分组能否从一种弯道类型迁移到另一种。

Conclusion: 在制动与策略维度的可重复性得到证明之前，不能将车辆视为可互换，否则会把本已很小的样本进一步切分为更小的单元。由于样本规模小、且放宽样本定义会改变跨车结果，本文结论属于初步结果，旨在作为个性化改进建议系统的分析基础。

Abstract: This paper presents TRACK (Telemetry-Based Racing Analysis and Coaching Kit), which is a framework for analyzing driving performance in sim racing and profiling how individual drivers behave behind the wheel. We report this framework together with its limitations: we calibrate each clustering result against a null, and when one does not separate from chance, we say so. Instead of restricting ourselves to scoring drivers or sorting them into preset labels, we represent each recording session as a compact geometry in a four-dimensional behavioral space (speed, braking, strategy, and consistency), and we group these fingerprints by their similarity using unsupervised clustering. Over time, we have developed and refined this framework on the open Assetto Corsa Gym (ACGym) dataset. Our study suggests that corner types differ along a behavioral dimension that was not used to define them. It also suggests that when the car changes, only speed and consistency carry over in the restricted population, while repeatability could not be shown there for any of the braking or strategy measures. Cluster separation becomes less distinct as the range of available telemetry widens. Until that repeatability is shown, grouping on the braking and strategy dimensions cannot treat the car as interchangeable, which divides an already small sample into smaller cells. It is also not clear whether a driver's grouping carries over from one corner type to the next. We also normalize each metric against a reinforcement-learning reference agent. The reference does not depend on the sample, so the scale does not shift when the sample does. We intend these results as an analytical foundation for a personalized improvement suggestion system. The sample is small. The cross-car result changes when the sample is defined more broadly. These outcomes are preliminary.

</details>
